# Current Formal Frontier Toward Proving or Disproving the Riemann Hypothesis

## Research map of HTGeometry, HilbertMetric, and RHTower

**Corpus cutoff:** 12 August 2026, committed snapshot
**b65b29e6f476b24f1491e5f8a4d1f39dd1c45474**.

Formal status means theorem declarations reachable from the committed HTGeometry, HilbertMetric,
or RHTower umbrellas at this snapshot. A proof present only in a paper card is marked
paper-proved or provisional. A Lean file not reached by a committed umbrella is not counted as
formal frontier. The map records present state, not research chronology.

Results outside this commit receive formal status only when reached by a committed project
umbrella. Committed but umbrella-unreachable modules are recorded as such, and paper-only claims
remain paper-proved. The cutoff includes umbrella-formal P-G80--P-G90; Suzuki's canonical shifted-
Xi/meromorphic-inner receivers, canonical compact Mellin kernel, completed `H^log` boundary form
core, and Abel/log-Fourier equation-(2.6) bridge; Route L's Steinberg and Gamma-six normal
transgressions; Route U's effective, nodal, countable rational-node, and literal adaptive
arithmetic-source landings; the Sonin--physical-codiagonal seam; and the coefficient-faithful
Helson Fredholm wall. The cutoff additionally makes Suzuki's literal arithmetic Mellin/Xi-ratio
source, Route U's conditional nodal-supply pricing, the node-refinement no-go, and the signed
complex-phase Sonin splice umbrella-formal. Route P's P-G91 physical Pluecker covariance is a
committed module with a current uncommitted umbrella import; it receives no cutoff-formal credit.
Route T's fixed- and arbitrary-centre logarithmic-phase receiver modules are committed but
umbrella-unreachable. Generic rectangular diagonal/Schatten and bounded-perturbation Friedrichs
modules are likewise committed but unwired and receive no route credit. No running foreground
process counts as certification.

## Abstract

This paper maps every proof architecture toward the Riemann hypothesis currently instantiated by
the corpus. Routes are quotiented by their first unresolved source theorem, not by shared
vocabulary or final RH criterion. Each dossier states endpoint, proved mechanism, obstruction,
open core, and retired subclasses. Lean declarations are authoritative; paper claims are
separated from formal ones.

Neither RH nor its negation is proved. The independent live routes are C1, D2, E, G, L, O, P, Q,
R, T, and U. The unlettered CCM-FU lane is also live. Route N is terminalized: its final
zero-fracture premise is RH-equivalent. Routes T and U are new independent
architectures exposed by the enlarged corpus:

- **Route T** converts the mass-shifted Xi divisor geometry into one exact critical outgoing
  current and then into a finite centered von Mangoldt polynomial inside a moving mass light cone.
- **Route U** follows the first loss of positivity in the shifted-Xi Hankel tower and identifies
  its crossing form with a completed Euler--Gamma extension of an adaptive null flag.

These are not renamed versions of existing routes. Route T has a first-order critical
von-Mangoldt flux endpoint, whereas E has squarefree Möbius parity transport and terminalized N
has a prime-normal domain classifier. Route U has an adaptive finite Hankel null-face crossing endpoint,
whereas G seeks a cofinal Suzuki two-angle meromorphic carrier and P seeks one compact positive
moment measure.

No twelfth live lettered route is instantiated at this cutoff. Five possible Route-V
architectures now have nontrivial cores: conservative Euler-shell/Witt scale completion,
triptic orbital-divisor normal form, tropical-projective source-normal descent, and relative
formation-completion current, plus coefficient-faithful Helson Fredholm-wall response. The first
lacks its literal jointly convergent source inverse pair; the second now has an exact additive and
multiplicative kernel theorem but no coefficient-faithful Xi landing or critical reflection law;
the third has a prime-clock projective-width selector but no Xi-typed observer/descent theorem.
The fourth has an umbrella-reachable centered-jet/evenization classification, but no literal
arithmetic relative extension or source law annihilating its hidden current.
The fifth has a trace-class coefficient-faithful Helson realization and a nonzero simple-wall
crossing covector, but no canonical Xi coefficient stream, fixed RH threshold, or global wall law.
The reciprocal-character virial and inverse-response Ward laws give a concrete possible producer
inside the existing C1/CCM/Gauss architecture, not a sixth independent candidate route.
Umbrella-formal Green-square analysis also suggests a possible future quotient of P into C1:
both endpoints meet at positive spectrum of the same source-built `B^2`, with all nonpositive bulk
localized to one rank-two boundary current. The present Lean comparison reaches the P endpoints
through RH equivalences and does not construct a direct source-to-measure factorization, so P is
not merged at this cutoff.
The newly formal adelic Plucker-history, interaction-spectrum formation wall, essential-formation
quotient, passive-cycle law, and static-heterogeneity observer also do not instantiate Route V.
They give exact source mechanics, including the prime reflection wall at exponent one and
invariance under summable compensators, but no theorem identifies physical off-center Xi
displacement with their formation parameter or lands their neutral selector in a fixed Xi fibre.

The newest committed theorem layer changes several internal frontiers without changing that count.
Route U now reduces RH to a countable rational-nodal family and identifies each member with one
literal adaptive rational--Gamma-minus-von-Mangoldt landing, including its moving-admission
connection law. Route G's `H^log` boundary core and premise-free equation-(2.6) bridge and Route
P's full physical diagonal-transfer no-go are umbrella-formal. Route T's committed unwired chain
proves an explicit strict one-dimensional logarithmic-phase power saving, and the committed
unwired Sonin splice retains a nontrivial reflection-odd phase on one common cofinal schedule.
Route L now has exact half-density Floquet energies, an exact reflected-graph
radical, a universal diagonal-renormalization sign wall, an analytic Gamma-six orientation/residue
classification, and a source-first Mobius successor blow-up. Route O's completed-Evans branch now
has weak Gamma-tail domain coercivity and an exact root-port observation Gram. Route E now has a
formal rational-prime/Beurling comparison proving that every finite multiplicative regularization
of Mertens-normal log-jet Grams is zero-set blind. These are new mechanisms, receivers, and no-gos;
none supplies the still-missing source theorem of its route or creates an additional route.
Their first open problems remain respectively literal orientation of U's completed arithmetic
landing, G's closed operator/source admission, P's non-diagonal cumulative-incidence Feshbach
sewing, and T's MV24.11--24.12 near-one transport. A current Helson/Fredholm wall theorem
supplies a substantial candidate architecture, but without a canonical Xi coefficient stream,
fixed RH threshold, or global wall-orientation law it is not yet a twelfth route.

two-parameter averaged-Chowla statement implies the exact uniform correlation input, vanishing of
the fibre off-diagonal, and `MRBilinear`; no maximal-over-truncations premise or numerical assembly
remains on that bridge. The alternative Gallagher bridge bypasses dyadic maximal windows and

`OffDiagonalSmall mu hh` simplifier proves `MRBilinear` directly.

## 1. Reading rules and status vocabulary

**Endpoint** is a proved implication or equivalence whose conclusion is RH. It is a receiver, not
evidence for its premise.

**Open core** is first unproved source statement after all proved connectors are removed. An
assumption equivalent to RH is not called progress.

**Closed mechanism** means corpus proves advertised construction cannot provide required
information. Closure is scoped: it does not refute every future mechanism using similar language.

**Positive survivor** is strongest nontrivial theorem exposed by a failed route: rigidity,
classification, exact boundary, or replacement design constraint.

**Independent route** requires a literal source object, an unconditional chain to an RH endpoint,
and a first unresolved producer not identical under the corpus's source-isogeny comparisons to an
existing route.

The live independent count is eleven lettered routes plus CCM-FU. This count is conditional only
on current corpus identification: future comparison could merge routes, and a constructed
post-J principal-relative current would add another.

## 3. Common source architecture and merger verdict

The corpus constructs a source atlas far beyond classical scalar criteria:

1. Wold activation gives zeta ratios and logarithmic derivatives.
2. Squarefree breadth and prime-power depth are separated before scalarization.
3. Möbius depth differences, Fisher damping, primitive von Mangoldt promotion, and Gamma
   whitening are explicit.
4. Regular and singular source-to-Xi landings retain divisor and multiplicity jets.
5. Reflection is tracked before positive energy, determinant, or scalar frequency fusion.

Several apparent routes share one horizontal obstruction. Route R's graded determinant anomaly,
Route L's monodromy/Green obstruction, and the CCM Bockstein are source-isogenous realizations of
one reflection-odd horizontal class. Increasing vector dimension, Kummer depth, or route count
does not raise this atlas rank. The common operation previously missing was:

~~~
SOURCE-ZERO-TO-HORIZONTAL-CARRIER:
construct a source-derived relation which retains and orients the horizontal class before
bounded scalarization, while preserving prime labels, divisor multiplicity, and both reflection
orientations.
~~~

`SourceZeroHorizontalCarrier.lean` closes this operation without bounded scalarization. For every
Xi-divisor multiplicity copy it pairs complete-resolution reflection displacement with the full
ordinary-prime Gamma Bockstein family. Ordered adjacent source ratios recover the exact horizontal
grade; both components reverse under `rho ↦ 1-conj rho`; source-tail log times every prime
component is strictly positive exactly off the critical line. The physical Rees--corona landing
retains prime, depth, divisor, and multiplicity provenance.

This completes carrier construction and orientation, not annihilation. Lean proves global carrier
vanishing equivalent to RH. The pairing of the two retained cofinal orientations is now itself
constructed and signed: weighting the product of the tail-log and prime-Bockstein odd components
by the assembled Gauss residue current gives the Hodge mass current
`H(q,p) = (log 2/2)·current(p)·density(p)·t_q·(p^{t_q} - p^{-t_q})`, `t_q = 2 Re q - 1` —
unconditionally nonnegative, reflection-even, label-faithful, with exact critical radical
(`GaussHodgeMassCurrent.lean`). The Hodge sign of the constructed pairing therefore holds; zero
total mass of this nonnegative current is proved equivalent to RH. A formalized interface
firewall shows the mass-zero law cannot follow from the pairing's own interface even with
divisor-reflection closure: an explicit reflection-closed off-line configuration has positive
mass. The seam-energy bridge (`GaussHodgeSeamEnergyBridge.lean`, with the hyperbolic
comparison layer in `HTGeometry/HyperbolicBounds.lean`) then proves the Hodge mass current and
the Burnol FE--Schwarz seam reflection energy are one invariant: two-sided domination with
explicit constants (`log p/2` and `2√p/log p`; every divisor copy is proved to lie in the open
strip), pointwise co-vanishing, and RH ⟺ prime-2 seam-charge vanishing ⟺ prime-2 Burnol
character unimodularity ⟺ full seam-energy vanishing on the divisor. Because the corpus's seam
layer already evaluates the full prime sum — seam energy at a copy equals the convexity second
difference `M(1+2σ)+M(3−2σ)−2M(2)` of the moment curve `M(β)=Σ_p (log p)² p^{−β}` — the
common open core is now exactly one **horizontal divisor transform**: the divisor average of
that convexity defect. A formalized information-gap theorem fences it: the FE-pair sample of
the prime character is the constant `1` and no function of it computes the Hermitian sample
`‖char‖` — the missing evaluation lies strictly outside the holomorphic sample algebra
(the `Σg²` vs `Σ|g|²` gap as a theorem). Known fencing no-gos: density envelopes cannot decide
the horizontal transform, and its bilinear upgrade lands on diagonal-vs-off-diagonal
separation — the MR/correlation wall. External anchors: Suzuki's screw function
(arXiv:2206.03682) and Connes--Consani Sonin-space Weil positivity (arXiv:2006.13771).
`GaussHodgeCoherenceBudget.lean` then prices the residue exactly: the divisor is
conjugation-closed and `char(ρ)·char(conj ρ) = p^{2Reρ−1}` — the missing transform is the
conjugate-pair diagonal of a squared explicit formula (the pair-correlation classification as
a theorem) — and a Cauchy--Schwarz coherence budget makes the Hodge/seam mass an exact
certificate for zero-side exponential-sum coherence, collapsing to square-root cancellation
`2·|F|` under RH. The successor gate is a limit-exchange statement: vertical time-averaging
extracts the (multiplicity-squared) diagonal for finite families via the completed-time torus
layer; what is open is the interchange of that mean with the divisor closure, priced by
multiplicity squares (zero-simplicity layer) and fenced by the truncate-first co-Poisson
no-go. That interchange is now itself PROVED (`GaussHodgeVerticalExtraction.lean`): for every
ℓ¹ weight and every unimodular mode family — no gap, separation, or correlation input — the
Cesàro mean-square converges to the equal-mode pair diagonal (dominated convergence over
coefficient pairs), instantiated over the full divisor with the vertical flow at the
ordinates; the reflected partner shares the ordinate and the reflection-odd 2×2 block of the
extracted diagonal is exactly the cosh defect. The single unproven step left in the whole
chain is sourcing the ℓ¹ vertical pencil `Σ_ρ v_ρ·char(ρ)·e^{i n Im ρ}` from prime-side data:
the vertical mode is a conjugate-pair (bilinear) sample, and the information-gap theorem
proves no holomorphic channel supplies it. `GaussHodgePencilSourcing.lean` then closes the
loop on that step: the vertical mode has a holomorphic FE companion `e^{ρ−1/2}` whose pencil
IS prime-side data (`char_p·(e^{ρ−1/2})ⁿ = char_{p·eⁿ}` — the zero current at flowed scales),
whose polar factorization exhibits the sourcing defect as the radial mass factor `e^{t/2}`,
and **sourcing ⟺ RH** exactly (`riemannHypothesis_iff_feMode_eq_verticalMode`); under RH
every vertical-pencil value collapses to explicit-formula data. The Gauss/Hodge/seam
programme is now a single closed equivalence loop with every link an unconditional Lean
theorem; its one non-circular entry point is an independent (necessarily bilinear) evaluation
of the vertical pencil. `GaussHodgeWeilPolarizationFusion.lean` fuses the loop with the
corpus's Weil-positivity interface: at the prime exponential test the Weil pair summand of
`xiWeilPolarization` is constantly 1 (blindness, in-interface), the pair-square law holds for
every test over each reflection pair, the sharp-difference square of the prime test is the
cosh defect, hence the H-mass density is exactly the per-pair gap between the Hermitian pair
energy and the Weil polarization value, RH ⟺ that gap closes at prime 2, and the eigen-Gram
of the polarization is unconditionally supported on conjugate pairs with value
multiplicity·XiC′(α)·conj XiC′(conj α). A Gate-II entry — compressed-trace positivity matched
to `xiWeilPolarization` — now lands directly on the H-mass. The remaining content of this entry
is a source-derived global compressed-trace/intersection identity whose positivity controls the
Hermitian pair energy, not merely the blind Weil pair sample.
`GaussHodgeWeilGNSSocket.lean` makes the handoff exact: RH is equivalent to a global coordinate
square realization of every square-summable diagonal Weil value, and such a realization is
equivalent to Hodge-mass vanishing. The converse realization constructed under RH is tautological,
so this is a receiver socket, not a square-realization theorem. The missing producer is an
unconditional source-derived square/GNS or compressed-trace realization on the actual multi-place
class-space object.
`GaussHodgeWeilKreinDecomposition.lean` executes the direct reflection-pair construction and
classifies its exact obstruction. Unconditionally
`4·Re W(F,F) = Σ‖pair sum‖² − Σ‖pair difference‖²` on square-summable tests. The negative
sector's prime-test density is exactly the Hodge/cosh mass, and its annihilation for every test,
the square realization, Hodge-mass vanishing, and RH are equivalent. Thus indefinite Krein
realization is unconditional but not a producer: the direct pair-sum GNS subroute leaves precisely
the RH-bearing negative sector. A Gate-II theorem must annihilate that sector through an
independent arithmetic class-space law, not repackage it.
`GaussHodgeWeilFundamentalSymmetry.lean` names that sector's geometry: the parity flip
`J F = F ∘ conj` is an unconditional fundamental symmetry of the Weil form — J-self-adjoint,
involutive, with J-twisted form equal to the divisor ℓ² inner product — so the GNS Hilbert space
a realization would build already exists as the J-metric ℓ². Divisor parity is W-orthogonal;
the even sector carries an unconditional square realization, the odd sector is exactly minus ℓ²;
the diagonal Weil form is real. RH is the degeneracy law: RH ⟺ every divisor-odd test vanishes
on the divisor ⟺ W equals its own J-twist. The producer target is thereby minimal: any exogenous
positivity that forces J-twist invariance of W closes RH; nothing weaker than killing the odd
ℓ² sector suffices.
`GaussHodgeWeilEntireDetection.lean` minimizes the producer's burden: a multiplicity-exact
entire witness (normalized terminal divided quotients at an off-line point and its reflection)
shows the rough test class adds nothing — RH ⟺ Weil positivity on entire square-summable tests
alone, ⟺ entire divisor-odd tests vanish on the divisor, ⟺ parity-twist invariance on entire
tests. Any producer controlling the analytic class (explicit formula, compressed trace, CC
compression) therefore controls everything; no positivity on non-analytic tests is ever needed.
`GaussHodgeWeilDeviationMoment.lean` and `GaussHodgeWeilSymmetryInsufficiency.lean` close the
producer question from both sides. The deviation moment identity factors the negative sector
exactly: Re W(F,F) = Σ‖even‖² − Σ (β−1/2)²·‖ΔF‖² (deviation × divided difference), with the
unconditional strip bound Re W ≥ Σ‖even‖² − (1/4)Σ‖ΔF‖², and RH ⟺ the deviation moment
vanishes. The insufficiency theorem proves the source cannot be symmetry: an explicit even,
conjugation-real entire comparison function with strip-placed off-line zeros carries an entire
test of negative Weil diagonal, so any producer must consume the Euler product. The open core
in final form: an arithmetic inequality dominating the deviation-weighted divided-difference
moment by the even energy on entire tests.
`GaussHodgeWeilArithmeticDeviationGap.lean` makes the Euler product act on the Weil form for
the first time: PNTPlus's zero-free region (3-4-1 positivity) plus the functional equation bound
every zero's squared deviation strictly below 1/4 — by a uniform arithmetic gap at height ≤ 3
and by the de la Vallée-Poussin log⁹ margin above — improving the deviation-moment lower bound
to Re W ≥ Σ‖even‖² − (1/4 − δ)Σ‖ΔF‖² on low-supported tests. Calibration: known arithmetic
positivity repels zeros from the strip walls (weight < 1/4); the socket needs the weight at 0.
That remaining distance is the lane's exact open problem.
`GaussHodgeWeilTotalDeviationMass.lean` compresses the wall to one number: the total
normalized deviation mass Σ'(Im α_q)²/‖α_q‖² converges unconditionally (genus-one Hadamard +
strip), and RH ⟺ it equals zero. The all-heights arithmetic ceiling (uniform gap below height
3, log⁹ margin above) bounds every squared deviation strictly below 1/4 and yields the
unrestricted weighted deviation-moment bound for the Weil form and the ceiling-weighted cap on
the mass. The lane's entire content is now: known mathematics traps the mass in [0, ceiling·Hadamard];
RH is the statement that it sits at the left endpoint. This theorem family is committed and
umbrella-reachable; it sharpens the endpoint but does not supply the arithmetic law forcing the
mass to vanish.
`GaussPositiveCubicCriticalMass.lean` closes a tempting trace substitute negatively. The literal
folded Gauss block is a positive operator; positive-moment log-convexity amplifies its retained
first Fourier coefficient into the cubic diagonal. For three components, both reflected
orientations eventually dominate a fixed fraction of the critical sampled energy, every cubic
diagonal term is nonnegative, and the partial sums diverge to `+infinity`. Thus an unrenormalized
positive cubic trace cannot be the missing finite annihilator. Any global Gauss trace law must be
signed, relatively renormalized, or cancel through a distinct class-space differential.
`GaussReciprocalCharacterAdmission.lean` supplies a second exact normalization: the Hodge mass is
the virial derivative of one reciprocal-character split energy, has a quadratic centered-rate
lower bound, and its all-prime family is summable exactly at centered rate zero. Universal
inverse-response Ward mechanics shows what could force such a positive virial current to vanish:
a common inverse formation whose mixed source acceleration is balanced by its boundary anomaly.
The strict invariant-trace specialization is grade-blind, so any viable Gauss producer must be an
anomalous/source-relative Ward identity, not an ordinary trace. No theorem constructs this
arithmetic inverse formation or proves the required compressed-trace admission; the summability
condition itself is exactly RH. This is a sharpened C1/CCM/Gauss entry mechanism, not Route V.
Route L asks for the common horizontal annihilator through the resolved-Green
rational-Witt/Milnor source annihilator; Route R asks for it through singular Cartier/Stokes
non-amplification. These target realizations remain distinct even though they retain the same
horizontal class.

The new longitudinal--transverse conservation law explains why this common atlas does not itself
select. Normalized source resolution lives in the zero-codiagonal radical; every intervention that
preserves that radical leaves the divisor coordinate unchanged except for a predeclared scalar
action. A merged architecture therefore needs an independently source-generated nonzero mixing
defect before physical landing. Nonzero mixing is only necessary: it must still preserve labels,
multiplicity, reflection, and the route-specific admission law.

The residue-promotion anomaly makes this constraint sharper. Attaching a new law cell preserves
the existing residue exactly iff the attachment already annihilates the defect. Any compensator
has rank at least the anomaly rank; a fitted rank-minimal compensator splits off as a contractible
summand and creates no new law. Coordinate-stable compensators are infinite-dimensional, no
holomorphic compensator can reproduce the physical mixed defect before divisor specialization,
and the pre-reflection charge has separation rank exactly two. Thus a viable R/L/CCM comparison
must be genuinely mixed and source-relative before specialization; finite rank-one or holomorphic
repairs only re-present or erase the obstruction.

Umbrella-reachable `SoninCofinalReporterClockCommutatorSeam.lean` supplies one exact cross-route splice. On a common
`ell2` source, positive Sonin pullbacks converge strongly to the identity; the transported complex
Fisher score is reporter term plus an explicit clock commutator, and that commutator vanishes on
each fixed source in the cofinal limit. A diagonal sequence lands exactly on Route E's literal
prime-labelled one-corner codiagonal, with common limit `2u/(1+u)^2`. For every `u>0` both sides
are eventually strictly positive. This is a real Sonin/E source identification and a negative
design law: after Fisher scalarization it cannot annihilate an off-neutral orientation. Any useful
splice must retain a reflection-odd or otherwise signed coordinate before this landing. Umbrella-
reachable `SoninCofinalComplexPhaseOddFisherSplice.lean` constructs that upstream coordinate. Along
every inward complex ray it proves the exact holomorphic critical Fisher profile
`2u/(1+u)^2`, retains the unitary logarithmic phase before real scalarization, and on one common
cofinal finite schedule subtracts the `+it` and `-it` orientations. The limit is the explicit odd
profile `4it(1-t^2)/(1+t^2)^2`, which vanishes exactly at the neutral class for `|t|<1`. This is a
genuine source-orientation producer at Sonin/squarefree level. It does not identify that odd score
with the completed Xi physical metric or prove an RH-annihilating global law, so it remains cross-
route infrastructure rather than a new proof architecture.

This does not merge C1, D2, E, G, L, O, P, Q, R, T, and U into one serial super-route. Route N is
terminalized and contributes a receiver/diagnostic invariant, not another producer. The live routes
have different admission and producer theorems. R/L/CCM comparisons cannot be counted as independent
evidence merely because they use different target geometries.

The remaining problems are therefore not mostly missing connector lemmas. They are source
selection, source-relative coercivity, singular boundary annihilation/positive closure, or
genuinely oscillatory all-prime cancellation before information-losing completion.

## 4. Route dossiers

### Route A — literal CCM first clock [TERMINALIZED]

**Endpoint.** The completed first-clock scalar is nonnegative exactly under its RH orientation.

**Closed chain.** Fixed source-faithful physical-phase transports are identity. Every nonidentity
transport has an explicit phase-connection defect. Aperture-dependent positive Markov transports
exist exactly when the original source scalar is nonnegative; universal feasibility is RH.

**Verdict.** Route A is closed as an independent source-producing mechanism, not as a proof of RH.
First-clock tail bounds, Bohr restatements, and positive mixers cannot reopen it.

**Positive survivor.** Phase-natural rigidity and barycentric orientation completeness provide
exact tests for any proposed successor.

### CCM-FU — four-unit source-codiagonal incidence [OPEN]

**Endpoint.** A moving Gamma/Sonin/Hodge incidence carrying the continued Xi Ward column with
nonincreasing four-unit energy yields the required Ward orientation.

**Proved chain.** The squarefree Fisher lower corner has exactly four critical energy units.
Critical source converges weakly but not strongly, escapes every fixed compact synthesis, and every
internal Xi jet has a source-defined square-summable physical horizon.

**Open core.** Construct a prelimit cofinal noncompact family joining that corner and physical jet
horizon to continued Xi Ward column while preserving source provenance and four-unit budget.

**Dead subclasses.** Fixed compact operators, abstract RKHS realizations, and postlimit
codiagonalization are formally inadequate.

### Route B — fixed-width rectangular Weil floor [RETIRED]

Finite rectangular source, completed lag, prime wall, and reconstruction are extensively
formalized. Positive quadrature and fixed-width scalar transport do not supply a source
orientation. Any successful variable-width or relation-valued replacement changes primitive and
is a new route.

### Route C1 — complete Xi--Weil / Hedenmalm positivity [OPEN]

**Endpoint.** Positivity of complete Xi--Weil form on faithful singular domain implies RH.

**Lean-closed chain.** Corpus contains Hedenmalm source Weil form, folded Dirac defect, and exact
antidiagonal Gram law. Source involution `f(x) ↦ conj(f(-x))` is involutive and realizes the
Xi--Weil sharp operation after source Fourier transform. Theta-cusp seed has exact zero mean.
The transform-convention bridge, eigen-pair line integrability, far-right von Mangoldt line-series
swap, Xi-derivative substrip decay, and generic widening-rectangle vertical-edge vanishing are
formalized and umbrella-reachable. The pair-line product is now exactly one Fourier transform of
an `L1` convolution; scaled Fourier inversion evaluates it at the prime frequencies. The
single-pole fixed-aperture rectangle identity and the concrete right vertical-edge limit are also
formalized.

Umbrella-reachable Lean proves that the fixed theta source has every real exponential moment, so
its shifted-tail condition holds at every complex parameter. It uses the compact Hilbert--Schmidt
source inverse `B` and constructs
the correctly signed entire determinant

~~~
thetaSourceEntireDet2(z)=det₂(I+zB).
~~~

The credited files prove, on the whole plane,

~~~
thetaSourceEntireDet2(z)=0  ⇔  XiC(z)=0,
~~~

with no spurious point spectrum. At every determinant zero its analytic order equals the
algebraic multiplicity of the reciprocal eigenvalue `-z⁻¹` of `B`. Source construction, all-shift
admission, exact divisor identification, and Jordan/generalized-eigenspace accounting now receive
formal credit. Scientific missing statement is not spectral realization; it is a source-derived
reason that the nonzero spectrum of this concrete nonnormal compact operator is real, equivalently
that all `XiC` zeros are real.

The resolvent chart is now complete, but it does not create another C1 producer. For every fixed
shift `X>=0`, Lean proves

~~~
RH  iff  Re xiResolventHankelForm(N,a,X) >= 0
          for every finite degree N and real coefficient vector a.
~~~

Every resolvent moment is unconditionally positive once `X>=(n+1)^2`; every Hankel form with
one-signed coefficients is positive once `X>=(2N+1)^2`. Thus any negative witness in that sector
must have both coefficient signs. Its possible negativity is bounded by one `ell2` imaginary mass
`M(X)`, with `M(X)<=2H/(X+1)^2`, and `M(X)=0` is itself equivalent to RH. The corresponding
complete-monotonicity signs hold for all orders on the quadratic far ray. These declarations
localize the obstruction but do not force `M(X)=0`; they belong to Route U's shifted-Hankel
architecture and leave C1's real-spectrum/positive-closure theorem unchanged.

The umbrella-reachable chain also proves the global function identity

~~~
thetaSourceEntireDet2(z)=XiC(z)/XiC(0),
~~~

constructs finite-dimensional Riesz/Jordan root-block calculus for every nonzero source spectral
point, and builds an absolutely convergent, cutoff-independent essential inverse-flow trace equal
to the fixed-width Xi--Weil polarization. Its logarithmic samples are the literal prime--Gamma
coefficients, and boundedness of one positive-width orbit is equivalent to RH. These are exact C1
connectors and receivers, not an independent producer.

The squared theta-boundary Cayley clock is also umbrella-reachable and proves

~~~
RH  iff  its fixed rank-two observed orbit has subexponential growth.
~~~

Under RH the Cayley spectrum lies on the unit circle; failure of RH supplies an expanding divisor
mode. This is an exact source-defined dynamical receiver, not a mechanism forcing the growth
bound, and therefore does not change C1's producer core.

The umbrella-reachable unbounded-operator branch realizes the closed weighted derivative as the graph inverse of the compact
centered source inverse, proves dense domain, trivial kernel, mean-zero range, Fredholm index
`-1`, constant adjoint kernel, and literal `C_c^infinity(R)` graph core. For every complex
`alpha`, the same chain constructs `A+alpha` on the unchanged domain and core, proves closedness,
injectivity, closed range, and compact inverse, and proves the associated Witten Hamiltonian
`(A+alpha)†(A+alpha)` closed, densely defined, self-adjoint, nonnegative, kernel-free, and with
compact zero-resolvent. The shifted-cokernel modules now identify the whole range as the
orthogonal hyperplane of one explicit conormal and prove

~~~
thetaCokernelFunctional(sourceForcing) = -i XiC(alpha),
sourceForcing in range(A+alpha)  iff  XiC(alpha)=0,
unique shifted source solution  iff  XiC(alpha)=0.
~~~

They also prove Fredholm index `-1`, the exact adjoint-kernel line, and the explicit source
representative of every closed-operator solution. The supersymmetric block
`Q=[[0,A†],[A,0]]` is now constructed on its natural product domain and proved self-adjoint with a
compact resolvent at `i`. Its kernel is the pinned constant line, its range is the orthogonal
hyperplane, its Fredholm index is zero, and the underlying odd differential has Witten index
`-1`. Thus shifted cokernel, source equation, and supersymmetric operator geometry are formally
closed; real divisor spectrum and positive chiral transmutation are not.

A larger direct-Cayley/two-port cluster is now umbrella-reachable. It works directly with `B`
rather than `B^2`, proves another observed-growth RH equivalence, gives the complete
source-pencil resolvent row, Esscher cyclicity and exterior resolvent frames, the singular-metric
rank-two Green commutator, and exact phase-linear welds to Route T. These declarations sharpen
observability and transfer but still do not force a growth bound or real spectrum. They are a C1/T
cross-route receiver, not Route V.

Umbrella-reachable `HedenmalmThetaGreenSquareBoundaryLaw.lean` exposes a C1/P seam. If `G` is
the singular positive theta metric, `B` the fixed theta source inverse, and `R_2` the pinned
rank-two current, the formal identity is

~~~
G B^2 = B^* G B + i R_2 B.
~~~

The first term is a positive bulk square and the correction has range in the pinned affine
two-dimensional boundary space. The same module proves that every nonzero spectral value of `B`
has strictly positive real square iff the nonzero spectrum of `B` is real, hence iff RH; by
composition with existing capstones this is also equivalent to Route P's compact Markov and
Thorin properties. Pointwise neutrality of the source-built boundary current on every
multiplicity-exact divisor mode is likewise formally equivalent to RH, squared-spectrum
positivity, and both Route-P endpoints. These exact receivers localize a shared candidate producer
to neutralization of `i R_2 B` before scalarization. They do **not** yet merge
C1 and P formally as routes: the property equivalences pass through RH and no theorem constructs
the P measure from a source neutralization or proves every possible P producer factors through
this Green square.

Umbrella-reachable `HedenmalmThetaGreenReflectionPairNoGo.lean`
closes the obvious functional-equation pairing shortcut negatively. Reflection preserves theta
root energy and negates the signed rank-two boundary flux, so reflected-pair neutrality holds
unconditionally. Adding it to genuine same-fibre neutrality is still exactly RH. Any C1/P
producer must therefore neutralize each divisor fibre before reflection aggregation; quartet or
pair cancellation cannot supply the missing sign.

**Parallel explicit-form connector, one Lean assembly remaining.** On the decisive eigen-pair class, the
prime plus Gamma pairing is identified with the antidiagonal Hermitian Gram by a pinned
single-pole Guinand--Weil rectangle argument. This closes the prime-identification gate at paper
level, not positivity, and falsifies the prior wall-grade obstruction conjecture on this decisive
class. Lean no longer lacks Fourier convolution/inversion or the fixed-aperture/right-edge pieces.
The sole gap on this particular connector is widening-limit assembly: bottom-edge rewrite,
truncated-to-full-line passage, evenness folding, and final gluing. It is formal backlog, not the
scientific open core of the exact-determinant branch.

The CCM branch separately has exact nonunital strong cyclic quotient, closed restriction quotient,
descended Lefschetz action, Real structure, Clifford transgression, and nonzero Haar--Mellin
separator. Its algebraic adelic core is now identified exactly with the genuine rational
Bruhat--Schwartz space: one compact open finite-adele period, one compact support, Schwartz real
fibres, injective and surjective evaluation, and pointwise sign-reflection Real structure.
Untwisted Lefschetz action is Real-even; multiplication by i supplies canonical anti-Real
transgression.

The archimedean CCM source calculus is now exact through Connes--Consani's full `quantsmooth`
lemma. Umbrella Lean constructs the literal Hardy projection and quantized differential,
identifies its reflected Fourier crossblock with the positive-quadrant Hankel operator, and proves
that operator's approximation numbers decay faster than every integral power. The proof uses the
source smooth cutoff and all oscillator powers, then proves its positive compression equals the
actual Hankel block; trace class is only a corollary, not a substitute for rapid decay. This closes
a primary-literature gap but supplies no Weil positivity or Route-C1 spectral orientation.

The source's next analytic prerequisite is also closed.  Umbrella module
`CCMArchimedeanPhaseTemperate.lean` derives global all-order polynomial bounds for
`u_infinity(t)=conj(zetaChi(1/2+it))=exp(2 i theta(t))` from the vertical digamma and polygamma
bounds plus the exact normalized-Gamma phase ODE. Thus `hat f*u_infinity` is constructed as an
actual Schwartz function for every Schwartz `hat f`, and its quantized differential is
infinite-order. `CCMArchimedeanConvolutionRepresentation.lean` separately proves the exact Haar
law `rho(g*h)=rho(g)rho(h)` and the starred square law by Bochner/Fubini/shear arguments.
`CCMArchimedeanPhaseOperatorProduct.lean` proves the exact source Leibniz identity and makes the
tested spectral operator infinite-order, Schatten one, and trace class.
`CCMArchimedeanMellinTransport.lean` proves exact Mellin diagonalization of every intrinsic `L¹`
log kernel and transports this result back to the literal geometric operator
`rho(f)(1/2)(u_infinity^* d u_infinity)^g`, which is trace class for every Schwartz log source.
`CCMArchimedeanFourierMellinScattering.lean` further proves the exact Connes--Consani identity
`F_eR^w = I ∘ u_infinity^g` on the full even `L²` carrier. Its endpoint has no density,
integrability, Gamma-normalization, or intertwining hypothesis; those facts are proved on the
dense even-Schwartz core and extended by continuity. Therefore the live Proposition 7(iv) gap is
no longer phase regularity, representation product, Mellin operator identification,
Fourier--Mellin scattering, trace-classness, or Lemma `sch3` geometric kernels. Umbrella modules
`CCMArchimedeanGeometricScatteringKernel.lean` and
`CCMArchimedeanGeometricCommutatorKernel.lean` prove the literal kernels of `sch3(i),(ii)` on the
actual multiplicative-Haar carrier, including the sharp projection on the required even
`L¹∩L²` core. `CCMArchimedeanGeometricCompositeKernel.lean` proves `sch3(iii)` as an exact
operator/distributional pairing. It also exposes and closes a rigor point hidden by the source's
notation: the displayed pointwise composition integral is not Bochner integrable. At `ν=μ=1` its
tail is `-4 cos²(2πy)`; Lean proves its truncated positive mass tends to `+∞` and the literal tail
is not Lebesgue integrable.

The 12 August committed archimedean chain closes the previously named intermediate gaps.
`CCMArchimedeanGeometricScaledKernel.lean` proves `lemsch7(i)` distributionally;
`CCMArchimedeanGeometricSpectralBridge.lean` identifies the geometric composite with the Mellin
conjugate of the tested spectral phase differential; and
`CCMArchimedeanPhaseTraceFormula.lean` proves the literal Proposition 7(iv) spectral trace
identity. Independently, `CCMArchimedeanWeilDistribution.lean` constructs the finite-part global
archimedean Weil distribution, proves its integrability, and splits it into finite-aperture and
tail terms. Exact real, circle, Hankel, and quantized trace cocycles now avoid any diagonal-kernel

`RouteEFinitePhysicalCodiagonalGaugeCollapse.lean` now closes stronger composed geometry. When
`sourceCorner` is itself lower corner of unit-state pointed Fisher connection with tangent `chi`,

~~~
hsNormSq(sourceCorner) <= (1/2)||chi||^2,
|hsInner(sourceCorner, contractiveReporter)|^2 <= (1/4)||chi||^2.
~~~

For every all-order finite squarefree Euler history packet this improves raw gauge-collapse from
`sqrt(2)|sigma-1|sqrt(V)` to `|sigma-1|sqrt(V)`. Division-free gauge neutralization, valid even at

Separately, critical lower-corner energy is at most `4(1-exp(-u))` and has density four at `u=0`.
Abstract small-cutoff output is therefore `<=sqrt(2u)`. This is a genuine square-root receiver,

~~~
u_shell = 2 log ell / ell + O(1/ell),
~~~

so even the limiting profile has only `2 sqrt(log ell/ell)(1+o(1))` scale there. Dividing out the
Euler gauge makes the finite one-corner norm grow like `ell`; shrinking the tilt cannot create
cancellation. The new singular graph-normal source cancels this gauge exactly and is contractive

an outward parameter and the scalar observer has divergent `l2` norm. Both assertions, including

`RouteEStrictRightMellinObserverFirewall`. Hence no theorem identifies
the finite sharp Ramaré/unit-shell centered reporter with a strict-half physical corner. Revised
analytic/physical target is
`FINITE-PHYSICAL-COLUMN-ANOVA-STRICT-HALF-AND-SIGNED-RECURRENCE-SEAM`, not statewise flatness or
universal Fisher cutoff tuning. For literal pushed/target column masses `rho,nu`, the exact identity

~~~
chiSquare(rho|nu)
  = N * Var_(nu/N)(rho/nu) + (sum rho - sum nu)^2/N
~~~

is now an unconditional umbrella Lean theorem for arbitrary finite unequal masses, including empty
index type without a nonzero-total premise. It shows centered codiagonal energy is precisely
non-scalar column ANOVA. At `N=X^(1+o(1))`, its
endpoint target is `N*Var <= X^(o(1))`. Cauchy--Schwarz then puts centered score and atomic
codiagonal correction at unnormalized size `X^(1/2+o(1))`; maximum-state diffuseness is no longer
another power obligation. Scalar term is signed discrepancy itself and cannot be assumed small.
For normalized physical laws `alpha,beta` on relation `E`, minimizing this energy over
supported row kernels gives exactly `(A^2/B)d_E^2`, where `d_E` is target's weighted
chi-square distance to reachable marginal polytope. Finite projection duality gives

~~~
d_E = sup_f [sum_s alpha_s min_(t~s) f_t - E_beta f]_+
            / sqrt(Var_beta f).
~~~

Thus endpoint target is exact all-reporter expansion at X^(-1/2+o(1)). Exact normalized
weighted Hall gives d_E=0. A one-sided density ceiling 1+epsilon gives only
d_E^2<=epsilon, sharply, so generic 1+o(1) unit Carleson is not rate-sufficient. A target
partition gives exact within-sector plus between-sector ANOVA; for two seam sectors latter is
(g-b)^2/(b(1-b)). Seam cannot be optimized away and restored later.

This projection-duality and strict-half rate firewall is dependency-closed and formalized in
`route-e-weighted-hall-anova-projection-duality-law`. Umbrella-reachable
`RouteEWeightedHallProjectionDuality.lean` contains the compact convex reachable-marginal set,

The same umbrella-reachable module supplies the sharp cut obstruction. If a source subset has

projectionDistanceSq >= delta^2/(b(1-b)).

Thus strict-half projection energy forces every nontrivial physical cut deficit to be
`X^(-1/2+o(1))*sqrt(b(1-b))`; normalized Hall expansion is quantitatively necessary at the same

Lean-checked layer-cake strengthening completes finite theorem: normalized weighted Hall is
equivalent to existence of exact supported transport and zero projection distance. Canonical

The physical conditional-projection layer is now exact. `RouteEPhysicalFibreCoarea.lean` builds
the original-law conditional kernel in every positive physical fibre and proves row stochasticity,

`RouteETwoSidedColumnLoadGradient.lean` proves `q=2/(1+ell)` for full rows,

A generalized-prime firewall now fixes what arithmetic specificity any successor must use. For an

Newest centered formulation is equivalent at producer level but has fewer obligations: group

Finite ternary marker analysis is now umbrella-formal.  Exact Lean theorems retain all

Grown umbrella theorem `abs_sum_sign_weight_le_degreeNormalizedReplacementDefect` opens an

|J_V(1/X)|

For actual nonnegative substochastic recurrence kernel `P`, let `r,ell` be row and column

E_P = sum_{n~m,n<m} b_nm exp(-m/X) (q_n-q_m)^2.

Thus direct producer is weighted-isolated mass `<=X^(1/2+o(1))` and

parity-agnostic reciprocal formula is candidate; must be closed on common physical aperture.
Supports need not reverse-match.

The third formulation conditions `m`-fold physical histories on their exact multiplicative

sum_o mass_o thetaGood_o^2

|log(W_X(n)/W_X(n'))| <= m H^2/(X(X-H)).

Product-history Gibbs stability is therefore no longer open; it does not prove parity mixing,

Its physical-fibre capstone then gives

Gibbs distortion and finite physical measure change inside a fixed product cell are therefore no

Umbrella-reachable `RouteEComponentHistoryShuffle.lean` makes the component-histogram proposal

cardinality does not imply parity mixing; the source must prove the actual histogram regime makes

Umbrella-reachable `RouteEComponentHistoryGibbsParity.lean` closes the last committed abstract

prod_c (1-2 k_c(true)/(m+1)).
~~~

On a common aperture `[X-H,X+H]`, physical Gibbs parity differs from this explicit bias by at most

~~~

Lean propagates this sharp defect through arbitrary histograms, the balanced-incidence absolute
bound, both squared-energy endpoints, and the singleton specialization. Thus neither finite

where `B` is total balanced
incidence mass `sum_c min(k_c(true)/(m+1),1-k_c(true)/(m+1))`; this replaces orbit size by
the exact arithmetic statistic a producer must make large. It does not prove that the ordinary-

**Blunt verdict.** Old statement “define `R_X`” is obsolete because `R_X^gr` is now explicit at

projection/all-reporter interval expansion, cross-anchor co-degree, and co-moving seam flux.
Direct recurrence-weighted inverse-load/column-load Dirichlet energy and product-fibre conditional
parity remain equivalent alternative interfaces for the same arithmetic burden.
In the third lane, exact target is now control of the displayed histogram-bias product (or its
balanced-incidence exponent) plus explicit Gibbs curvature, not an unnamed conditional energy.
Finite component-orbit counting, product-history Gibbs distortion, and Gibbs-to-uniform comparison
are formally closed on the third lane. All three live alternatives are now fixed arithmetic
quotient problems. Unquantified unit capacity, replica cardinality, signed flux decomposition, and
degree exponents are not enough.

- the first unresolved gate is earlier than paired spectral occupancy.  On the half interval write

Umbrella-reachable `SuzukiMovableAnchorClarkCoherence.lean` fixes the movable-anchor local
normalization: the Donoghue kernel is normalized at `ic`, one Clark atom has exact Nevanlinna
weight `(x^2+c^2)p/c`, and any orthonormal family has total normalized source coherence at most
one. Hence an `N`-mode family contains a mode of coherence at most `1/N`; a uniform positive
source-coherence floor bounds mode count. This is a sharp fragmentation constraint, not the
cofinal pointed-law producer.

Umbrella-reachable `SuzukiTwoPortProjectiveWedge.lean` gives the complementary projective
mechanism. Common ground multipliers cancel projectively: the transverse wedge is
scaled only by the square of the multiplier. For the literal antipodal decomposition
`beta=e^(iax)u+e^(-iax)v`, its formal aperture derivative has leading wedge
`-2ia(u wedge v)` plus four transport terms, with an exact lower bound whenever that leading term
dominates the remainder. This removes scalar gauge from the desired projective motion. It does not
prove that Suzuki's actual Hardy ports possess the required derivatives or remainder domination;
that analytic port-regularity interface remains part of the fixed-anchor theorem.

The positive survivor is now one necessarily singular theorem, not two. It no longer needs to be
stated through a moving cyclic vector: compactified Krein spectral shift of the paired canonical
extensions contains exactly the same data. Xi-node rotation, local rank growth, and spectral
pollution appear there as pole--zero gap occupancy. Coalescing adds no second Xi-node problem, and
the source-impedance selector removes its zero-energy edge debt once the occupancy law holds.
Generic strong resolvent convergence still cannot prove the required trace hierarchy.

**Source-form parity status.** The literal 2026 restricted-Weil test form is no longer
blocked at finite-part semiboundedness. `SuzukiRestrictedWeilFinitePart.lean`,
`SuzukiRestrictedWeilSemibounded.lean`, and `SuzukiRestrictedWeilComplexCore.lean` prove the exact

literature-parity stack starts at completion and representation: construct closed localized
complex form, actual `A_a` and `B_a`, their Friedrichs equality, actual lowest eigenvalue and its
continuity/simplicity/even sector, then deficiency and characteristic objects. The committed
snapshot now includes `SuzukiScrewNondegeneracy.lean`, which identifies the literal interval form
with the operator pairing and proves nondegeneracy iff injectivity, plus the generic

content of Suzuki's boundary approximation (3.2): the literal smooth endpoint cutoffs converge to
each zero-extended aperture mode in the exact logarithmically weighted Fourier energy.
`SuzukiBoundaryLayerFormCore.lean` upgrades this to the actual completed form domain: complex
smooth tests and endpoint errors have genuine form vectors, each zero-extended aperture

restricted-Weil form. `SuzukiCanonicalShiftedXi.lean` also proves the exact
2012 proposition

~~~
RH iff every positive symmetric shifted-Xi function A^omega has only real zeros.
~~~

`SuzukiCanonicalShiftRatio.lean` adds the literal `E^omega`, its sharp reflection, and
meromorphic `Theta_omega=E^omega sharp/E^omega`. It proves the regular-locus reciprocal identity,
unit boundary norm, and exact equivalence between Suzuki's Hermite--Biehler inequality and
holomorphic regularity plus `|Theta_omega|<1` in the upper half-plane. Totalized division is handled
honestly at poles. This fixes the literal two-angle meromorphic receiver; it does not prove the
Hermite--Biehler inequality in the critical parameter range.

`SuzukiLagariasMeromorphicInner.lean` closes the exact 2012 meromorphic-inner proposition:

`SuzukiCanonicalArithmeticMellin.lean` now closes the literal arithmetic source side of Suzuki's
2012 construction. It defines the inverse-Mellin dilation sources `H` and `H_1`, proves their
finite floor-truncated equations (204) and (a204), justifies the required Tonelli/Fubini and

**Formal full-grade Steinberg normal transgression.** The counterterm's source coefficient
is no longer unspecified.  At every depth `m`, the complementary rational pair

~~~
x_m = 3^m/(2^m+3^m),    y_m = 2^m/(2^m+3^m)
~~~

is an honest Steinberg pair and has ratio `x_m/y_m=(3/2)^m`.  In the rational Kummer group
algebra, let `P_m=([x_m]-1)([y_m]-1)`, let `N[u]=(log u)[u]`, and put

~~~
q_m(T)=T(T-L_m)(2T-L_m)/(a_m b_m (a_m-b_m)),
a_m=log x_m, b_m=log y_m, L_m=a_m+b_m.
~~~

The exact coefficient-only identity is

~~~
[y_m]^-1 q_m(N) P_m = [(3/2)^m]-1.
~~~

Its horizontal character is `(3/2)^(m(2 Re(s)-1))-1`, exactly the full-depth successor
reflection anomaly.  Under the half-density Deninger representation it is

~~~
(T_(2->3,m)-I) h_m = R_(3,m)-h_m -> -Theta.
~~~

successor edge are one source object.  This is not an ordinary Milnor endomorphism or Hochschild

Steinberg ideal.  General tangent-K/additive-K background is literature-known; the

successor are project-specific. This closes the successor-coordinate normal but not the actual

**Formal direct Gamma-six normal transgression.** The successor clock mismatch is now
removed rather than repaired by a fitted scalar.  The finite Möbius correction

~~~
H_(6,m)=R_(1,m)-Theta

satisfies, for every `m>=3`,

The honest Steinberg pair

~~~
x_m=6^m/(1+6^m),   y_m=1/(1+6^m)
~~~

has normal extraction `[6^m]-1`.  Reversing the normal orientation swaps `x_m,y_m` in the
same unoriented cell and extracts `[6^(-m)]-1`, so the oriented source boundary is

~~~
[6^m]-[6^(-m)].
~~~

Its horizontal character is exactly

depth `m-1`, `6^m` is the literal modular clock; at `m=1` this is the fixed actual
Gamma/Burnol/BV coefficient up to its already fixed positive calibration.  Both orientations

~~~
[6^m]-1=(1-[6^(-1)]) sum_(j=1)^m [6^j],
[6^(-m)]-1=(1-[6]) sum_(j=1)^m [6^(-j)].
~~~

The oriented Kummer regulator is `2m log 6 != 0`. Hence ordinary cyclic descent cannot close

physical source. On the complete corrected tail,

~~~

while any reciprocal landing `T_(6,m)^(-1)K_m -> Y != 0` forces

satisfies `Xi(s)B_6(s)=A(s)+Xi(s)P_6(s)`, where

### Route O — Connes restricted-Weil/prolate ground space [OPEN]

**Route-level endpoint.** Let `lambda_0(a)` be the bottom of the closed restricted-Weil form on
`L2(-a,a)` and put

~~~
eta(a)=max(0,-lambda_0(a)).
~~~

Nested apertures make `lambda_0` nonincreasing and `eta` nondecreasing. Nonnegativity of every
true floor is equivalent to RH. A cofinal limit `lambda_0(a)->0` also suffices by monotonicity, but
is stronger than needed.

**Lean-closed finite and comparison layers.**

- literal finite restricted-Weil matrix, reflection, real-rooted completion, and selected floor;
- nested-floor monotonicity and no-evacuation: one negative trial vector pins all later floors;

  envelope;

  polar source, endpoint energy, full primitive, and dilated endpoint laws.

  bridge
  ~~~

  Q_L(a)^-<=2L eta(a);
  ~~~
- false-RH transverse amplification of the actual negative floor and the exact sampled criterion
  ~~~
  RH
   iff eta(a)=exp(o(a))
   iff sigma_abs(sum_n eta((log n)/2)n^(-s))<=1.
  ~~~
  More quantitatively, every right-transverse Xi displacement forces cofinal exponential growth
  of `eta` and lower-bounds the sampled Dirichlet abscissa.

This advances formal status beyond “concrete radial PSWF realization missing.” Core
special-function primitives, exterior channel, inward regularization, sharp trial-to-floor
domination, transverse amplification, and sampled-growth consumers are Lean-closed. Lean currently
uses the exact variational lower-bound/positivity interface; it does not reconstruct the published
closed CCM operator itself.

**Completed-Evans/log-seam subbranch, formal.** `SuzukiGammaMollifiedEquation.lean` and
`SuzukiGammaWeakDomainUpgrade.lean` close the weak regularity step that this branch previously
treated as two independent derivative-scattering charges. For a source-supported tested equation
with `kappa_sigma+|tau|<2L_0`, mollification and the exact shifted-Gamma numerical-range bound give
`U in L2` and `V in H1`; derivative charges are conclusions, not premises.
`SuzukiRootPortObservationGram.lean` gives the exact finite observation identity

||P T beta||^2 = (sum_i w_i^2)(||beta_-||^2+||beta_+||^2),
~~~

with prime-power weight sum `sum (log p)^2/p^j`; once `a>(log 2)/2`, every nonzero endpoint trace

**Finite primary-literature parity correction.** The former conditional
`CCMZetaSpectralTripleFinite.lean` hypothesis-packaging file was deliberately deleted after the

eta(a)=exp(o(a)).
~~~

Equivalently, prove the sampled abscissa bound above or exclude negative spectral birth at every

    has the already-proved bounded divided boundary value.
32. The valuation-`>=2` von Mangoldt correction is absolutely convergent and holomorphic on

    cannot cancel that pole. Wold-to-divisor identification is therefore closed.

    two-sided comparison with

    physical defect is equivalent—not merely implied by—the normalized consecutive-prime-gap

    summability; improving the same quadrature estimate cannot bypass it.
34. The complete four-shift Wold divisor is now formally identified as a cubic translation

    transports terminal even root-jet mass `gamma_r` to odd energy `r*gamma_r`. Nonterminal
    divided jets have zero odd energy and distinct roots remain orthogonal. Under full-ground
    nonnegativity every nonterminal odd descendant is therefore an operator-kernel vector, giving
    `dim ker A_sigma >= 1+2E` for `E=sum_r(m_r-1)`, with sharper additions from terminal zero mass
    and a root at zero. A simple full ground forces every root simple, every terminal mass positive,
    and every squared root strictly positive. The source-specific paper proof and strengthened
    parent card are committed at this cutoff. Literal hard-edge compactification, both parity form
    domains, and cancelled root division are not Lean formalized, so no formal credit is assigned.
55. The finite ancestor of negative hard-edge root signature is now exact. If `x_i` is a selected
    flat-face quadrature node at mass `y`, then its hard coordinate is
    `u_i=x_i^(-1)-y`, not `x_i`. The finite polynomial form
    `E_y(q)=L_y((1-yX)q^2)` has inertia equal to the counts of nodes below, on, and above the
    moving apex `1/y`; on a root quotient its value is `(1-yx_i) gamma_i=x_i u_i gamma_i`.
    After the actual far-right normalization this is exactly the first Hausdorff-difference matrix
    `[m_(i+j)-m_(i+j+1)]`. Hurwitz therefore forces every negative hard root of multiplicity `m`
    to have at least `m` super-apex quadrature ancestors with
    `y(yx_i-1)->-r`. The same difference is the literal radial source flux
    `C_y/(k+1) d/dy[y^(k+1)s_y(k)]`. A positive atomic countermodel with one node
    `1+c/y` of vanishing weight proves that weak/fixed-degree arcsine recession cannot detect this
    leakage, even though the hard coordinate tends to `-c`. This is paper-proved in
    `mass-shifted-hard-edge-super-apex-hausdorff-inertia`; no Lean declaration is
    claimed.
56. Individual selected Gauss nodes now have an exact source evolution. In every positive
    degree-`n` chamber, with monic norm `h_n` and root-quotient mass `gamma_i`, the centered
    reciprocal coordinate satisfies
    `u_i'=2n h_n/(x_i^2 gamma_i)>0`. At a flat face whose first nonzero Padé defect is
    `delta_(2n+d)`, all derivatives `u_i^(r)` vanish for `1<=r<=d`, while
    `u_i^(d+1)=(-1)^d (2n)^(overline(d+1)) delta_(2n+d)/(x_i^2 gamma_i)`. Right-face positivity
    makes this last value positive for every node. Thus every source contact law signs a coherent
    hard-root jet but cannot anchor its absolute endpoint side. The complementary anchor is an
    exact residue law. The flat quadrature Padé resolvent has poles at `Y=-u_i`, positive residues
    `rho_i=omega_i/x_i`, and after hard normalization
    `rho_i=Gamma_i/[t_i^(2n+1) S'(u_i)^2]`. Along the selected limit, a simple root `r` satisfies
    `rho_i->gamma_r/F'(r)^2`. Hence a simple negative root with positive terminal mass forces a
    positive-axis Padé atom of nonvanishing mass even though its ordinary Christoffel weight
    vanishes. This is paper-proved in
    `mass-shifted-hard-edge-gauss-node-jet-residue-law`; no Lean declaration is claimed.
57. The surviving hard Padé residue now has a literal restricted-Weil source meaning.  If a
    simple negative hard root is `r=-kappa^2`, cancelled division by `z^2+kappa^2` produces a
    time-domain vector `eta_r` satisfying the weak source equation
    `A_sigma^+ eta_r=(gamma_r/F'(r)) cosh(kappa t)`.  After unit-source normalization, its energy
    is exactly
    `Cap_sigma(kappa)=gamma_r/F'(r)^2`, hence exactly the limiting positive-axis Padé residue.
    If `gamma_r>0`, `cosh(kappa t)` is orthogonal to the whole even ground space and
    `Cap_sigma(kappa)^(-1)` is its finite zero-edge reduced-resolvent response.  If `gamma_r=0`,
    the terminal descendant is a source-visible ground vector and that response has a pole.
    Under the regularization `A_sigma^+ + tau`, positive terminal mass is equivalently an `O(1)`
    Lorentzian capacity spike on the `sqrt(tau)` scale around `kappa`; zero terminal mass gives
    only `O(tau)` capacity even at the root.  Hence pointwise estimates away from the root and
    unscaled source averages are provably blind to the atom; a closing source estimate must rule
    out this precise square-root boundary layer uniformly.
    Thus Route U's two residue branches are the finite-impedance/ground-pole alternatives of one
    literal hyperbolic source, not separate Padé pathologies.  This is paper-proved in
    `mass-shifted-hard-edge-hyperbolic-source-capacity`; no Lean declaration is claimed.
58. The canonical Padé resolvent has an exact moving-center worldline.  At fixed absolute mass
    `Y`, `partial_y R_y(Y)=2n h_n(Y-y)^(2n-1)/D_y(Y)^2`; every pole moves left, its residue obeys
    an explicit pair-interaction ODE, and total residue grows.  The exact error against the
    completed-Xi source is `(Y-y)^(2n) H_y(Y)/D_y(Y)^2`, where every Padé pole is a transverse
    source zero with derivative `-x_i^3 gamma_i`.  This proves analyticity cancels rather than
    forbids the spurious pole.  The tempting literal radius bound also fails sharply: at
    `n asymp sqrt(y)`, the highest-moment absolute-divisor majorant for any bounded pole worldline

    center flow, analyticity, spectral-radius separation, and every absolute-divisor majorant are

    `mass-shifted-moving-center-pade-worldline-law`; no Lean declaration is claimed.
59. The simple-ground Fourier real-zero theorem is parity-free.  Connes--van Suijlekom prove the
    even case.  In the missing odd case, the first-order commutator does not control the Fourier
    resolvent, but its restriction after squaring the frequency operator does.  If `xi` spans a
    one-dimensional odd kernel, normalize `<D eta,xi>=1` and set
    `T'=D^2-|D^2 xi><D eta|` on the odd sector.  The commutator makes `T'` self-adjoint in the

    determinant is the numerator of the odd Fourier resolvent in the variable `z^2`; hence every
    Fourier zero is real.  Approximation and Hurwitz remove the parity assumption for every simple

60. The completed-Xi resolvent has an unconditional positive-real structure that survives the
    moving Padé chart, but it exposes a sharper boundary layer instead of closing it.  With

    disk residue `rho_tilde asymp 2 rho/y`, while the Julia-normalized residue satisfies

    `(y+c) sum_i rho_i (y+a_i+2c)^(2m-2)/(y-a_i)^(2m) <= G(y)` for `1<=m<=n`; at `m=1`, every
    atom right of `-c` must be financed by Padé mass left of `-c`.  But at `n asymp sqrt(y)` one
    fixed physical residue changes the whole matched disk jet by only `O(rho/sqrt(y))`, or

    This is paper-proved in `mass-shifted-pade-carathéodory-boundary-resolution-law`; novelty is

60a. Finite-head resolvent depletion cannot sharpen item 60 by a matched-jet or Carathéodory

60b. The Connes--van Suijlekom rank-two commutator forces an exact parity-multiplicity balance:
    at every eigenvalue, even and odd multiplicities differ by at most one.  The proof injects the
    source-dark codimension-one part of each parity eigenspace into the opposite parity by the

61. The fixed-shift resolvent-Hankel endpoint is now exact Lean. For every `X>=0`, positivity of

62. The unconditional sector is unbounded in degree. Every moment of order `n` has positive real

    `XiShiftedCanonicalAdmissionProjectiveNextAdmissionRenewal.lean` closes the first genuine

76. A dependency-closed paper theorem sharpens that cofinal synchronization wall. Admission-root

    d/dy ((2n+1)/F_n-y) >= 1/(2(2n+1))

    in the far-right shifted-Xi chamber. Thus absolute root concentration and continuous work
