**Committed polynomial Turan descent and one-point criterion.** The route has advanced past edge
cleansing. `XiResolventWeightedTuranMachine.lean` proves a universal weighted level-cleansing
machine. `XiResolventSpectralDescent.lean` uses real polynomials that vanish on higher real levels
to descend through every finite resolvent-modulus level and proves, at any one shift `X>=0`,

~~~
RH iff for every real polynomial p and every n,
  Re(M_p(n) M_p(n+2) - M_p(n+1)^2) >= 0.
~~~

`XiResolventEvenTuranMachine.lean` and `XiResolventSingleMinorCriterion.lean` remove the `n`
quantifier: polynomial multiplication by powers regenerates the needed even ladder, so the single
index-zero minor for every `p` is equivalent to RH. `XiResolventTraceCauchySchwarz.lean` packages
the same endpoint as

~~~
RH iff for every real polynomials f,g,
  Re(L(f^2)L(g^2)-L(fg)^2) >= 0,
~~~

where `L` is the explicit one-shift arithmetic resolvent trace functional. This is not positivity
on squares: the family is invariant under `L -> -L`, and proof mechanism is spectral oscillation
and polynomial annihilation of already-cleansed levels.

Plain unweighted minors are insufficient. `TuranMinorSubEdgeMasking.lean` gives an explicit
conjugation-symmetric nonreal configuration in the open right unit half-disk with every moment and
every plain consecutive Turan minor nonnegative. Higher real atoms mask the lower nonreal pair;
some source-selective reweighting is necessary. `XiResolventPartialLineSupply.lean` supplies the
`p=1` index-zero inequality only after all atoms above a scale are already real and one explicit
tail-mass versus separated-two-moduli inequality holds. It is a partial-RH bridge, not an
unconditional producer.

`XiResolventEffectiveTuranDetection.lean` and
`XiResolventEffectivePolynomialDescent.lean` make the cleansing finite: explicit gap, mass, and
weight data bound an index beyond which an off-line level forces a negative even minor, and
polynomial killing weights reduce the certificate to one index-zero minor. The terminal family is
smaller still. `XiResolventNodalSingleMinorCriterion.lean` proves, for every fixed `X>=0`,

~~~
RH iff for every finite R subset (0,1] and m>=0,
  the index-zero minor reweighted by (X^m product_(r in R)(X-r))^2 is nonnegative.
~~~

Thus arbitrary real polynomials are a valid receiver but not the minimal open quantifier. Monic
real-rooted nodal squares already detect every off-line level.

`XiResolventRationalNodeCriterion.lean` makes this minimal receiver countable. Uniform source-
moment continuity under node perturbation proves that it suffices to take a node count `j`, an
exponent `m`, and a rational tuple `c : Fin j -> Q` with every coordinate in `(0,1]`. Rational
density recovers the real nodal inequalities, and Lean records countability of the complete index
type. The terminal criterion is therefore a countable arithmetic inequality family, not an
uncountable positivity wall.

`XiResolventNodalSupply.lean` now prices each member of that countable family. If all divisor atoms
above a chosen resolvent scale are already on the line and an explicit two-scale tail-mass bound
holds, then the weighted index-zero Turan minor is nonnegative. For a rational nodal datum the
budget is explicit: its adverse tail cost grows as `2^(4j)` and its witness reserve contains the
degree factor `(r_1 r_2)^(2m+1)`, the squared node-distance products, and
`(r_1-r_2)^2`. This is a conditional source-supply theorem, not an RH proof: the corpus does not
yet establish its line-placement and mass hypotheses uniformly for every datum.

`HTGeometry/TuranMinorNodeRefinementNonmonotone.lean` closes a tempting shortcut. An explicit
conjugation-symmetric three-atom configuration has a nonnegative plain Turan minor but a strictly
negative one-node refinement. Nodal refinement therefore has no monotone order from which the
countable signs can be inducted; the family must be exhausted datum by datum or controlled by a
different global invariant.

**Literal countable arithmetic landing.** Umbrella-reachable
`XiShiftedNodalAdaptiveArithmeticSourceLanding.lean` closes the source-formation interface for
that family. For each weight `p`, the division-free successor

~~~
Q = L(p^2) (X p) - L(p X p) p
~~~

is orthogonal to `p`, and its mixed landing is exactly the weighted Turan determinant. At square
shift it is also exactly one common-kernel rational--Gamma-minus-von-Mangoldt packet formed from
`(p,Q)` before reflection aggregation. The moving-admission derivative is minus the literal
bilinear completed arithmetic source plus the exact admission-connection source. For every fixed
`w>1/2`, Lean proves RH equivalent both to nonnegativity of all real nodal landings and to
nonnegativity of the countable rational-node subfamily. These equivalences do not prove a sign.

**Hard-edge and flat-contact source laws.** The committed
`XiShiftedHardEdgeAdaptiveSourceTransversality.lean` identifies literal shifted-Xi Gauss/Padé
terminal mass as Padé residue times adaptive completed-source capacity.
`XiShiftedCanonicalAdmissionHardEdgeWorldlineSourceLaw.lean` strengthens this to an exact
worldline conservation law: reciprocal hard-root speed times terminal mass/residue/capacity equals
the source norm, bounded speed forces a lower terminal mass, and parabolic forcing tends to `4c`
when degree/shift tends to `c` and hard displacement is small. Along a cofinal admission, if degree times reciprocal hard displacement
tends to zero and the compact-normalized hard derivative tends to `d`, then effective source
capacity tends to `d^2`. At a simple compact hard root (`d != 0`) extinction therefore cannot be
blamed on capacity blow-up or collapse.

`XiShiftedCanonicalAdmissionFlatContactSourceTransmutation.lean` treats the tangential face. The
actual inverse-Hankel coefficient velocity is the degree-lowered Möbius tangent; every simple flat
Gauss root follows `x'=-x^2`, so its reciprocal hard coordinate is first-order frozen, while the
division-free norm flux differentiates to the adaptive completed source formed before reflection.
These laws preserve arithmetic forcing at a flat face but do not produce positive-chamber or
cofinal hypotheses from the global source.

**Revised open core.** Prove unconditional nonnegativity of the now-explicit countable
rational-nodal adaptive arithmetic landings, without assuming partial RH or an equivalent positive
functional. The missing theorem is no longer construction of a prime--Gamma/global-Xi landing or
an unspecified supply estimate: Lean has identified every target minor with its literal common-
kernel rational--Gamma-minus-von-Mangoldt formation and has given a quantitative sufficient supply
budget for every datum. What remains is to prove those line-placement/tail-mass budgets from the
source uniformly across the countable family, or find another source-side domination,
cancellation, or conservation law that signs every landing. Refinement induction is formally
impossible.
Adaptive null-face curvature price, projective work, hard-edge
worldline conservation, flat-contact transmutation, and adjacent-admission capitalization remain
possible producer mechanisms; none currently signs the rational-nodal landings. Older Padé/root-
sign and simple-birth/full-ground alternatives below classify ways supply can fail, not additional
route endpoints.

**Earlier equivalent structural formulation.** In the degenerate/root-sign branch, items
55--58 replace unspecified odd descent by a literal Padé spectral-measure and source-capacity
statement. Define
`mu_j^+=sum_(u_(j,i)<0) (omega_(j,i)/x_(j,i)) delta_(-u_(j,i))`. Prove
`mu_j^+(K)->0` on every compact `K` in `(0,infinity)`, or the equivalent source-side vanishing of
the Poisson boundary atoms. This is weaker than full first-Hausdorff positivity: it permits
super-apex nodes whose residues collapse or whose hard coordinates escape. It excludes every
simple negative hard root with `gamma_r>0` by the exact residue limit. If `gamma_r=0`, use the
resulting parity-doubled terminal radical instead. Full odd-sector operator admission would still
require liminf control on the complete odd form domain.  Equivalently at a simple limiting root,
exclude a finite nonzero zero-edge response of the literal source `cosh(kappa t)`; forcing a
ground pole collapses the Padé residue but must then be combined with the explicit degeneracy
contradiction.  Equivalently, rule out the `sqrt(tau)` Lorentzian regularized-capacity spike
uniformly on every compact positive `kappa` interval.  Generic reduced-resolvent positivity,
pointwise control off the roots, and unscaled capacity averaging do not decide which alternative
occurs.

Odd or degenerate Connes--van Suijlekom extension is also closed as a stand-alone mechanism.
Positive terminal mass makes `i kappa` common only to the even kernel; it does not constrain an odd
lower-ground vector there.  Moreover item 59 proves that a simple odd lower ground is itself
Fourier-real-rooted, so odd descent supplies no contradiction.  A viable use of the full-ground
theorem must first prove from the literal completed-Xi source that the selected even boundary state
is the full bottom, or derive a new cross-parity identity forcing its nonreal zero onto every lower
odd ground.

Item 60b further removes a hidden premise: whenever the positive-mass branch really descends below
the even floor, the odd lower ground is automatically unique and simple.  Its Fourier transform is
real-rooted and its reciprocal gap is one exact cross-source current, but neither invariant forces
vanishing at the even imaginary root.  Any successor invoking odd-ground degeneracy or merely
assuming odd simplicity is therefore obsolete.

The positive-real Padé lane is now equally sharp.  Item 60 proves that ordinary convergence in
capacity is too weak by one conformal derivative: it sees `rho_tilde->0` automatically while the
physical atom is the normalized quantity `rho_tilde/(1+z)`.  Carathéodory positivity supplies a
new exact reserve law, but a bounded positive atom needs only a vanishing fraction of the large
left-pole reservoir at the critical degree scale.  A closing theorem must therefore control the
Julia/Carleson boundary atom and its left-reserve payment using the literal prime--Gamma source;
no finite matched-jet or unweighted disk-capacity theorem can suffice.  Item 60a also closes the
obvious finite-head repair: enlarging the analytic half-plane inserts asymptotically identical
opposite boundary jets, so depletion has no stand-alone Carathéodory leverage.

Contact orientation is closed as a mechanism: every first Padé defect gives the same signed first
nonzero derivative to every hard node, but a positive derivative at a flat face does not make its
level nonnegative. In the simple-birth branch, the parent radical-jet theorem already merges the
problem with the literal simple-even ground sign; more root division, contact parity, or generic
Laguerre--Polya theory is theatre unless it produces a new prime--Gamma/global-Xi anchor. Fixed-
degree arcsine recession, weak convergence, and bulk quadrature cannot close either branch because
a vanishing ordinary weight may retain nonzero absolute Padé residue after hard blow-up.

The current-Christoffel outgoing-ray route remains an alternate producer,
but items 51--52 close its fixed-mass comparison and cancel all explicit admission velocity from
the mass-varying source debt. Item 53 proves generic positive moment flow cannot control the
remaining odd defect-energy drift plus current norm flux. Only a label-sensitive prime--Gamma or
global completed-Xi law can revive this lane. It is not logically required for the selected least-defect sequence. Fixed-face plateau,
amplitude gauge, coordinate gauge, sub/superparabolic least-defect escape, and translation to
spectral infinity are closed.

A Stage-1 successor proposes an alternate U-native formulation: the parabolic degree lower bound
puts terminal positivity within one bounded relative mass interval, so after static root division
is exhausted the remaining datum is adjacent frozen-to-current admission transport on that bounded
interval. Item 51 closes arithmetic formation and every fixed-mass comparison. Missing theorem is
now a literal arithmetic prime--Gamma/global-Xi quantitative or sign law for the item-52 odd
defect-energy/norm-flux expression along the selected path; generic positive resolvent-flow
arguments are false by item 53. It
is therefore a live refinement of the same parity-admission wall, not a new route or a proved
replacement open core; its abstract root-deletion wrapper receives no formal credit.

**Dead subclasses.** Generic moment positivity, positive atoms, tied-power sectors, positive/root
location, stochastic order, factorial-normalized carriers, fixed corona continuous reporters,
Gamma lifetime, per-orbit three-point positivity, fixed angular sectors, endpoint-height control,
reflection aggregation, unweighted total pair energy, and a post-contact fourth-cumulant reserve
all admit exact counterexamples, merely transport existing sign, or are equivalent to the terminal
sign. Absolute monic margins, amplitude-only normalization, coordinate-dilated recession, and
generic Gauss positivity likewise do not control moving admission shape.

**Why independent.** U is a first-crossing extension problem on an adaptive finite null flag.
Route G instead needs a cofinal two-angle meromorphic source and Xi transfer; P needs one global
compact positive measure. Neither route's producer implies U's adaptive Euler--Gamma crossing
orientation.

## 5. Candidate routes not yet instantiated

### Post-J principal-relative current

Would be new route only after literal source object, non-coboundary incidence, and unconditional RH
handoff are constructed. Current concrete realization is R.

### XiC order-one explicit-formula organ [SHARED INFRASTRUCTURE — NOT A ROUTE]

The former growth frontier is closed in committed umbrella Lean. `CompletedZetaGrowth.lean` proves
the unconditional global order-one envelope `xi_growth_order_le_one`; centered coordinate change
gives the corresponding XiC bound. `XiHadamardGrowth.lean` proves quotient constancy and the exact
multiplicity-faithful product

~~~
XiC = XiC(0) * xiCanonicalProduct,
~~~

with no residual exponential factor. `XiZerosInfinite.lean` proves the completed divisor support
infinite, while `XiHeatShellMinimum.lean` and the canonical-product layer supply shell finiteness
and inverse-square summability. Thus growth, Hadamard factorization, and nontrivial-zero existence
are not open.

The prime side is also umbrella-reachable: `XiWardInteriorTraceSplit.lean` proves on `Re s>1`
that `logDeriv xi` is its two pole terms plus `logDeriv GammaR` minus the von Mangoldt L-series;
`XiGuinandWeilSymmetricContour.lean` identifies the centered bottom edge with the exact
prime--archimedean source, and finite-contour modules give the zero-residue receiver. Moreover,
umbrella-reachable `XiRelativeResolventTrace.lean` already gives the absolutely convergent
centered zero-side partial fraction in relative-resolvent form.

Umbrella-reachable `XiExplicitFormulaLogDeriv.lean` transports that formula to classical
coordinates and proves, wherever `xi(s) != 0`,

~~~
logDeriv xi(s)
  = sum_rho (1/(rho-1/2) + 1/(s-rho)),
~~~

and glues it on `Re s>1` to the pole--Gamma--von-Mangoldt side as an unconditional zero-sum =
prime-sum identity. This direct classical-coordinate explicit formula is now formally certified;
it is shared infrastructure, not a missing proof route. Only a live route's independent
source-side positivity, coercivity, or cancellation theorem could turn these identities into RH
leverage.

Umbrella-reachable `XiWeilResolventChartDictionary.lean` now gives an exact base-chart weld. At
imaginary Cauchy parameter `-it`, each Weil pair
summand is the resolvent point at shift `t^2`; hence the diagonal Weil value is the zeroth
resolvent moment. Lean derives its strict positive real part, identifies it for `t>0`
with `logDeriv xi(1/2+t)/t`, and for `t>1/2` with the explicit pole--Gamma--von-Mangoldt
expression. These declarations receive formal credit. They orient only this Cauchy-test diagonal,
not the full Weil form or higher resolvent Hankel tower, so they weld C1/Weil and resolvent
infrastructure without closing a route or creating Route V.

Umbrella-reachable `XiResolventTowerLowDegreePositivity.lean` extends this weld formally. It places every shifted squared spectral point
in the open 45-degree cone for nonnegative shift and in the required 30-degree cone for shifts at
least `3/4`. Hence the first resolvent moment has strictly positive real part for every
nonnegative shift and the second does so for shifts at least `3/4`; an exact power-kernel
dictionary transfers these statements to squared and cubed Cauchy-test Weil diagonals. This is not
full Weil positivity: degree two at small shift and the higher tower remain unoriented. These
declarations receive formal credit but do not change any route endpoint.

### Order-one pre-Gamma Sonin successor

Would replace F's wrong-order finite-height transfer. No source operator and chain exist yet.

### Conservative Euler-shell/Witt scale completion

This is the closest new Route-V candidate at the cutoff, but it is not yet instantiated. Lean
constructs literal Möbius prime-exponent formation edges, their conservation/reconstruction laws,
polar criticality, and a bilateral-summability selector whose surviving radial character is
unitary. It also constructs pole-unit memory, the Gamma base, and the Euler-shell specialization.
The conservative inverse-completion theorem is exact: if finite formation and exterior companion
stages are inverse pairs and converge jointly in one Hausdorff multiplicative completion, the
inverse identity survives, so every multiplicative receiver sees a zero-free completed formation.
The literal pointwise Euler-shell specialization is now Lean-proved and classified exactly:
for every `m>=12`, pointwise conservative completion on the open right half-strip is equivalent to
RH. This is a terminal gate, not a source theorem; it deliberately forgets that the stages must be
source-formed and converge jointly in one completion.

`MarkedEulerShellSkeleton.lean` and `MarkedEulerShellWall.lean` strengthen the pre-zero source
side while proving a new obstruction. The marked exponent shell is coefficient-faithful on its
pure-prime skeleton, has a positive Mellin--GNS realization, compact resolvent, recurrent observed
orbit, divergent unwrapped history, and an HT realization. But no finite positive normalization
reaches the physical Mass half-density prime vector: shell summability requires `sigma>1`, while
matching `(log p)/sqrt(p)` forces `sigma=1/m<=1/12`; pure-prime reporting without the marked mixed
shell is not coefficient-faithful. Thus positivity, recurrence, and compact resolvent do not fill
the candidate's source-to-physical normalization crossing.

The missing source object is decisive: no committed theorem constructs the finite source-generated
inverse pair and proves its joint convergence on the half-plane needed by the endpoint. A further
no-go forbids hiding this in one ordinary Banach algebra with a bounded derivation satisfying
`D T=1`; the viable completion must be scale-, sheaf-, or locally convex. Until those literal
stages and their joint admission are built, this is a proved receiver architecture rather than an
independent route.

### Triptic orbital-divisor normal-form successor

Lean now gives a complete finite source quotient. Every mixed-constraint periodic meromorphic
source differs from an explicit finite elliptic-gamma jet correction by a differentially
algebraic meromorphic remainder, and every finite orbital divisor datum is realized. The additive
divisor is a finite class function with one jet basis element per orbit class and order. This is a
source-classification theorem, not merely special-function notation. The torus invariant-field
layer is also complete: arbitrary protocols retain velocity plus annihilating Laurent monomials,
while an expanding protocol leaves only a differentially independent velocity whose intersection
with palette rational functions is constant. The concrete presentation gap is closed as well:
elliptic-gamma cone and multiplicative presentations agree after the explicit coordinate shift,
meromorphic realization commutes with translation, logarithmic derivative, and every jet, and any
nonzero finite jet combination, including the untranslated base-source class, is formally
hypertranscendental. The remaining kernel ambiguity is now closed: a source is differentially
algebraic exactly when every additive orbital-divisor coefficient vanishes; for multiplicative
sources, its logarithmic derivative is differentially algebraic exactly when every triptic orbit
charge vanishes.

No committed theorem maps this quotient coefficient-faithfully to the Xi divisor or supplies a
reflection/polarization law forcing critical orientation. Those two constructions would instantiate
a genuinely new route because its first producer would be orbital-divisor exhaustion, not a
classical positivity or scalar criterion. At this cutoff it remains a second Route-V candidate.

### Tropical-projective source-normal descent

Umbrella Lean now gives a third Route-V candidate core. For any finite nonempty family of exact
source actions, mandatory maximum work has a nonnegative projective width; width vanishes exactly
when every parent increment agrees, is invariant under a common potential, and controls descent of
the full relative action profile through an observer. Closed histories have nonnegative work. For
finite prime clocks, arbitrarily late phase returns coexist with projective width growing at least
linearly with elapsed time and the logarithmic clock span. For a centered affine parameter and a
nonzero clock span, zero width is exactly the centered state.

This is not Route V. The centered-width theorem does not mention Xi, and the recurrence theorem
does not force observer descent. A route would require one source-normal Xi observer constructed
before divisor specialization, proof that the complete prime projective profile factors through
it at every Xi zero, and a coefficient-faithful identification of its zero-width fibre with the
physical centered state. No such observer, descent theorem, or RH handoff exists.

### Nyman--Beurling / Baez--Duarte finite sections

New Lean corpus formalizes criterion, Gram matrices, detectors, least squares, finite-section
determinants, Möbius residuals, and obstructions. This is important infrastructure but classical
receiver architecture. No new unconditional approximation producer was found; it is not counted
as Route V.

### Adaptive eigenframe source memory

Umbrella Lean now proves exact two-query Pauli twirling, Haar mean/tail laws, and permanent
fixed-body Majorana separation: for every fixed positive even body order, every nonzero autonomous
Hamiltonian with that support stays strictly above the Haar score at every time once qubit count
is large enough. The generator-matched form is stronger and dimension-only: every nonzero
traceless autonomous `n`-qubit Hamiltonian retains its full adjoint autocorrelation at every time,
whereas Haar probability of retaining even half that amplitude is at most
`4/((2^n)^2-1)`; the dictionary signal and Haar separation hold simultaneously. This is a genuine
autonomous source-memory mechanism. It becomes an RH route only after constructing an arithmetic
Xi-typed Hamiltonian or source, preserving divisor and multiplicity provenance, and proving a
coefficient-faithful handoff to an RH endpoint. None exists at this cutoff, so it is a candidate
grammar rather than Route V.

### Coefficient-faithful Helson Fredholm-wall response

Umbrella Lean contains a substantial candidate architecture. For every
divisor-summable coefficient stream `a`, `HelsonFredholmWall.lean` constructs the literal
trace-class Helson operator `K_a(j,k)=a_(jk)` on `l2`, proves coefficient faithfulness and
self-adjointness for real streams, identifies the quadratic response with the divisor convolution
`W_u(n)=sum_(jk=n) conj(u_j)u_k`, and uses the compact Fredholm alternative to characterize every
nonzero spectral wall. At a simple wall, the eigenvalue derivative in coefficient direction `h`
is `sum_n h_n W_u(n)`; the wall gap therefore has a continuous nonzero crossing covector. The
least active eigenvector coordinate supplies an explicit detecting coefficient at its square.
Normalized Mellin exchange tangents feed this crossing form directly. Generic compact-operator
wall language has thus become a coefficient-faithful arithmetic mechanism.

This is not yet Route V. Thresholds can be fitted to arbitrary eigenvalues, distinct eigenlines
give distinct response covectors, coefficient positivity does not imply operator positivity, and
the constant stream fails the required divisor summability. No theorem constructs a canonical
completed-Xi coefficient stream in this chart, identifies a fixed nonzero spectral threshold whose
avoidance is equivalent to RH, or orients/avoids every wall globally, including multiple
eigenvalues. The full entry/self-adjoint/response/wall stack is umbrella-formal. Promotion to a
route requires all four missing data:

1. literal source-derived Xi/prime--Gamma coefficient stream with proved divisor summability;
2. fixed RH-equivalent Fredholm threshold, not one selected after seeing the spectrum;
3. source law giving the crossing sign and excluding tangency/multiple-wall escape;
4. global continuation proving no forbidden wall is crossed from a known reference chamber.

Until then this is the strongest current candidate architecture, not a
twelfth live route.

### Longitudinal--transverse source selection gate

Umbrella Lean now decomposes each complete-resolution source into a marked longitudinal divisor
coordinate and a zero-codiagonal transverse radical. For every continuous linear intervention,
the output codiagonal is exactly its marked-section response plus a uniquely defined mixing defect.
Vanishing mixing is equivalent to preserving the radical; with scalar section action, such an
intervention can only rescale the already-present divisor. The literal complete-shell
specialization proves that arbitrary transverse perturbations leave the pole-unit scale response
unchanged.

This is a cross-route mechanism law, not Route V. It rules out normalized shell manipulation,
radical-preserving completion, or transverse source enrichment as a selector by itself. A new route
could begin only with a source-generated intervention having nonzero longitudinal--transverse
mixing, followed by reflected analytic admission and a coefficient-faithful Xi handoff. No such
intervention is instantiated.

### Cofinal port, inertia, and phase-memory receivers

New umbrella theorems identify limiting inverse capacity with admission, its zero set with the
closed vertical fibre, and closability with nondegeneracy; divergent weighted capacity gives dense
kernel. The prime port separates a finite zero jet from a divergent first prime-log jet. Complete
atomic fusion preserves the sum of Hermitian inertias, so negative channels cannot disappear under
full interpolation. Prime augmentation reduces to one hyperbolic plane and supplies no independent
Witt-rank obstruction. Finite lattice directions force rational verticality, but a cofinally
rotating lattice can converge to an irrational vertical line, excluding finite-direction
extrapolation. Finite unitary/Choi/Pauli and phase-action laws retain source memory, but an
alternating binary character reaches the phase maximum; phase complexity alone cannot select RH.
These results sharpen L/R/U receiver and no-go categories but have no Xi-typed producer and are not
Route V.

### Source-coherent multi-organ synchronization

Lean now proves a finite selection grammar that survives independent-organ gauge no-gos. Pairwise
relative responses reconstruct every local intervention modulo one global source action; one
anchor completes reconstruction. For a family of source response maps, synchronization selects
zero exactly when their difference equalizer is trivial. Combined with shared-gauge quotient
fusion, one global prime packet produces a nonnegative pairwise slope-variance defect conserved
through partitions and received by Boolean, mass, Hankel, and quantum-information charts.

This is not Route V. No committed theorem constructs Xi-typed organ maps whose divisor
admissibility forces synchronization, and no arithmetic proof makes their equalizer trivial on the
horizontal displacement. Those two source-first statements would instantiate a new architecture;
the generic finite theorem does not.

### Euler q-Gamma divisor-translation process

This is a real new source architecture but not yet a proof route. It begins with positive
prime-local q-Gamma laws, produces an ordinary random-integer law, continues its global response
meromorphically, and lands every zeta-zero multiplicity as the exact Cartier-divisor jump under
unit translation. The cyclic GNS observer is recurrent and has compact diagonal resolvent. The
response and its continuations are now Lean-proved hypertranscendental. The genus-one exchange is
also fully certified: renormalized local factors are quadratically close to one, their prime
product is multipliable, locally uniformly convergent, holomorphic and zero-free on
`Re(s)>1/(2 sigma)-1`, and the natural renormalization erases the translation divisor.

The abelian zeta-gas layer supplies its pre-zero source. Prime-power geometric modes give the
zeta-Gibbs law on finite abelian groups; order/temperature dilation and socle/fugacity translation
are distinct response axes, and the q-Gamma random-integer law is the exact socle shadow. Lean
certifies the coupled response ratio, depth-shift telescoping, depth-dilation semigroup, depth
condensation walls, local and global socle law, mode and gas expectations, and the additive
von-Mangoldt/`-zeta'/zeta` residue chain. The full response has a finite-depth divisor formula and
a convergent, nonzero, holomorphic depth tail, hence an exact meromorphic order formula away from
the natural accumulation boundary. It also proves that accumulation line is a genuine natural
boundary, constructs the gas word/KMS algebra with a unique admissible Gibbs state, and realizes
depth dilation as an injective RG embedding. On the class space Lean gives atomic mutual absolute
continuity, the canonical tilt density and Radon--Nikodym/log-density formulas, measurability of
the class map, equality of class law with the gas pushforward, and socle expectation transport.
The entropy layer is now closed too: log-socle and both log-likelihood ratios are integrable, both
directed Kullback--Leibler divergences are evaluated, and their symmetrized value is exactly
`sigma` times the von-Mangoldt Dirichlet mass, equivalently the corresponding logarithmic zeta
derivative expression. The umbrella-reachable squarefull-sector module identifies the depth-two
wall at `1/2` and constructs the corresponding almost surely finite random abelian-group law;
this is a source phase boundary, not a zero-location theorem, because both order and activity
observers remain divisor-blind. The Gibbs Gram kernel, positivity on `a* a`, product-reversing star,
completed GNS representation, generated C-star algebra, positive cyclic vector state, and its
continuous normalized KMS uniqueness on the whole generated C-star algebra are now all
umbrella-reachable. The exact modulus analysis is now also formal at umbrella level: the complete
nonnegative depth-by-harmonic defect ladder is summable, equals the q-Pochhammer/q-Gamma
log-modulus defect, has leading term `q(1-cos theta)`, and differs from it by a remainder summable
over primes throughout `sigma>1/2`. Vacuum-weight bounds give probability at least `q/2` to one
copy empty and the other occupied, and prove these symmetrization probabilities are not summable
for `1/2<sigma<=1`. This is the exact Borel--Cantelli divergence input, not yet its product-measure
wrapper or a zero-location theorem. The modulus-budget module is now umbrella-reachable: phase
gauge leaves modulus unchanged, while the genus-one counterterm pays the leading defect up to an
absolutely summable correction for `sigma>1/2`. The wired prime-window theorem proves PNT in fixed
multiplicative/phase windows, divergence of the harmonic window sum, and nonsummability of the
leading defect for every nonzero phase when `0<sigma<=1`. The now-wired continuation transfers
this divergence to the full q-Gamma defect, proves its partial sums tend to infinity, the
phase-gauged product norm tends to zero for `1/2<sigma<=1`, and no continuous centering limit can
remain normalized at phase zero. These results supply a genuine
positive operator-algebraic source and a sharp probabilistic/analytic continuation boundary, not
a critical-line orientation.

A new law-control layer extracts a marked source commutator from this gas. Lean now proves that
exchanging temperature translation with source-selected socle tilt generates the pure fugacity
parameter translation. Lean also proves the socle response quotient, its exact translation law,
safe-chamber holomorphy/zero-freeness, hypertranscendence, and complete finite-order divisor law.
Positive fugacity entropy, recurrence/open lift, and any critical-line handoff remain open. The
mechanism is explicitly not RH.

A second comparison prevents overgeneralizing that commutator mechanism. The gas realizes
law genesis by noncommuting endpoint schedules, whereas the elliptic-gamma source has commuting
endpoint translations and trivial pure two-shift Koszul defect but a nonzero mixed
coefficient/Čech transgression. The proposed common grammar must therefore retain execution
exchange and coefficient/admission lift as separate grades. The two-source capstone
`graded_source_genesis_noncollapse` is now umbrella-reachable: it proves noncommuting gas schedules
and commuting triptic translations coexist with a non-coboundary gamma-gerbe `3`-cocycle. It has
no typed Xi handoff. This is a field-level architecture constraint, not a new lettered route.

What is missing is not another landing or special-function theorem. No source law relates these
two observer axes, recurrence, compact resolvent, hypertranscendence, or translation divisor to
`Re rho=1/2`. Until a source-derived critical-line orientation and unconditional RH handoff are
constructed, this remains Route-S infrastructure and is not Route V.

### Gauss quasi-inner multi-place assembly

This candidate has advanced from a formal finite-place sketch to a complete local asymptotic
mechanism. For each Gauss component the umbrella constructs the actual circle operators, proves
uniform moving-amplitude translation, exact principal factorization, controlled finite iteration,
and convergence of the quasi-inner diagonal coefficient to its closed period average along every
cofinal integer-frequency sequence. The full scaled two-frequency diagonal is now
umbrella-reachable. The sandwich fold and relative-diagonal bound isolate the principal quadratic
form with a vanishing dimensionless defect. The exact local logarithmic residue for the actual
operator in the rank-one case is now umbrella-reachable. For `m>=2`, the umbrella proves the
ratio-weighted moving-translation defect for every decay exponent above `1/2`, so the former
tail-exponent wall is closed. It also constructs the literal principal step, proves the exact
Fourier-block identity for its powers, and supplies the general operator-power telescope. The first propagated-iterate depth is now closed in umbrella Lean: the relative one-step law and
the double-defect estimate hold for every `m >= 2`, and the operator logarithmic-mean residue law
holds at `m = 2`. The remaining local theorem is propagated-iterate control at depth two and
beyond (`m >= 3`). A potential
new architecture would assemble these component laws over all places into the actual
Connes--Consani intersection/Weil pairing and extract a positive or oriented global source law.
The label-faithful multi-place assembly now exists in umbrella Lean: a block-diagonal operator
on the `ℓ²`-direct sum of the per-place circle spaces with identically vanishing cross-place
matrix elements at every power, per-label localized residue laws realized by the actual
operators at `m ≤ 2`, exact codiagonal collapse onto `gaussPairedChannelResidue` (so the paired
Schatten layer is the label-blind shadow), sample-value conjugation and phase evenness retained
per label, a canonical injective cofinal prime aperture, the Weil normalization
`residue·log p = (2/m²)((p-1)/p)^{2m}(p^m-1)²`, and a strictly positive residue current in the
horizontal carrier's `(Primes -> R)` label bundle that preserves the carrier's sign and critical
radical at every label. The assembled current now has its local Hodge pairing:
`GaussHodgeMassCurrent` combines tail-log and prime-Bockstein odd components into a
reflection-even nonnegative mass with exact critical radical and proves total vanishing equivalent
to RH. Thus local positivity/orientation is closed. What remains absent is an independent global
trace/explicit-formula identity forcing this positive mass to vanish, together with identification
of that identity with the literal Connes--Consani intersection/Weil object. The interface itself
cannot supply the identity: Lean constructs a reflection-closed off-line configuration of positive
mass. This is a strengthened C1/R/L/CCM architecture and still not a new route.

### Cassini--Wold / Deninger lower-half weld

This umbrella-reachable architecture is a real opening, but route-equivalence is already proved.
The Stieltjes--Cassini defect is a finite nonnegative sum that vanishes
exactly under RH; its phase-clock frequency is the mixed horizontal-grade/physical-height source
jet, and its BV--Wold landing is an exact positive Fisher energy. At critical half density, a
bounded Euler coframe removes the apparent compact Wold smoothing. The valuation-one Route-T
current then equals a pure Wold half-density current plus a uniformly bounded trace-class
correction, and the two line-bound propositions are equivalent.

Ordered Abel summation splits the pure Wold current into an explicit logarithmic-mesh current, one
static first-prime holonomy atom, and a residual connection. The residual arithmetic primitive
tends to zero with an exponential square-root-log rate. Its square is summable against the exact
critical harmonic prime weight, so it is an unconditional finite-Fisher-energy vector; pointwise
damping is still too weak for total-variation closure. Independently, the unsquared theta
resolvent row and singular-metric two-port Green identity express the same current; theta-Green
plus adjacent-prime/Wold current cancels to a bounded dispersive background with an explicit
two-residue future carrier. Exterior reflection does not yield a second equation: reversing the
safe theta line and frequency negates the complete packet exactly and preserves its norm.

The physical observer is now classified. Its coefficient `p+1` is unbounded on the Fisher carrier,
so residual-only bounded same-carrier landing is impossible; nevertheless it is exactly the legal
lower contour shift by `-i/2`. The induced residual connection converges absolutely and is
`C^infinity` with every polynomial log moment. Negative-frequency polarization now extends it
holomorphically through the entire lower half-plane, and division by the coordinate gives a global
Hardy bound. The main logarithmic mesh is an exact right-endpoint quadrature whose
error reduces to the classical mean-square prime-gap sum.

This does not instantiate Route V. Lean now proves the exact two-sided mesh criterion: at critical
depth, absolute mesh-defect convergence is equivalent to the weighted consecutive-prime-gap
energy. That critical energy monotonically controls the whole complex mesh-defect series on
`-1/2 <= Re s <= 0`, including every shifted zero in `1/2 <= Re rho <= 1`; imaginary height changes
only a fixed prefactor. Formalizing the published mean-square prime-gap theorem would therefore
close the entire mesh half-strip input, but not Route T. Lean also proves the exact safe Laplace
symbol, holomorphy of every valuation
`>= 2` contribution on the critical half-plane, meromorphic continuation of the valuation-one
Wold carrier, and a nonzero residue at the translated location of every off-critical zeta zero;
later shifts and higher valuations cannot cancel it. Surviving scientific work is source-side
continuation/noncancellation of the complete prime-zeta-minus-mesh-minus-holonomy identity toward
`Re s=-1/2`, or the equivalent finite-stage source/divisor cancellation through the cofinal
limit. The reflected two-line scalar quadrature is closed and should not be pursued.

The former two-sided residual shortcut is closed negatively. Its critical endpoint graph norm
would force the same prime-gap energy while opening the residual half-strip, but item 51 proves
that combination impossible because it erases genuine critical Wold poles. The published
prime-gap import is therefore not optional on any surviving absolute-mesh lane.

An umbrella-reachable Abel-threshold module closes the local bridge from
prime-gap moments to this mesh. If the prefix gap-square moment grows like `p_M^theta` for any
`theta<3/2`, the critical energy is summable; the concrete `theta=11/8` specialization closes the
whole complex half-strip mesh. Thus the only literature-to-Lean debt in this component is the
published unconditional consecutive-prime-gap moment itself in the required prefix form. Neither
supplies the remaining Hardy/Wold noncancellation theorem.

### Tight-source coalitional / collective-repair architecture

The new universal-law theorems expose another possible producer grammar. Positive-carrier
attainment can be attacked by proving tightness or bounded proper pricing for finite source
coalitions, while semantically conservative repairs can create a positive circulation only through
an irreducibly collective threshold invisible to each repair separately. A new RH route would have
to define the arithmetic defects and repair interaction before observing zero locations, prove the
tightness/threshold law from the prime--Gamma source, and map the born circulation
coefficient-faithfully to an RH endpoint. None of these arithmetic objects exists in the corpus.
The cofinal viability layer adds a sharp promotion law: compact observer fibres and closed coherent
horizons make finite survival promote to one global source witness; failure forces every witness
out of every compact set and makes every predeclared proper source cost diverge. Compact legal
control fibres also make the endogenous predecessor commute with the cofinal intersection and
produce the greatest viable fixed law. This rules out false finite-horizon immortality as a
producer unless noncompact escape, nonclosed admission, or incoherent horizons are used. No
arithmetic observer, horizon, or Xi evaluation is instantiated. This is a route-design opening,
not Route V.

The new symbolic-execution capstone closes another false promotion. Executable grade-`m` repair
always implies the local failure inequalities, and for one failure kernel the converse holds. On
the three-kernel triangle, however, uniform weight `n` is locally admitted at grade `2n` but cannot
decompose into `2n` legal grade-one repairs; these nonexecutable grades are unbounded while the
normalized profile and parity observer stay constant. Thus local Hall/failure inequalities plus a
bounded normalized observer do not certify an executable arithmetic source law. Any route from
this opening must build the actual compatible decomposition/history, not only its symbolic cone.
No such arithmetic execution system or Xi handoff exists, so the theorem narrows the candidate
grammar rather than creating a route.

The primitive-execution refinement now gives the exact surviving invariant. Every positive
locally admitted repair has a canonical primitive decomposition; bounded grammar is equivalent to
bounded primitive degree. Triangle repair is generated in degree two, but the cofinal
complete-complement family has primitive degree `k-1`, so every bounded grammar misses an explicit
admitted law. Primitive degree spectrum is invariant under coordinate relabeling and under the
failure-antichain quotient. The exact primitive ray executes, its projective profile and cyclic
grade observer are periodic, yet its exact grade advances without bound. Thus a viable arithmetic
execution route needs a cofinal primitive spectrum or genuinely unbounded grammar; bounded
observer data cannot promote it. No prime--Gamma repair family or Xi handoff is instantiated, so
this is a sharper route-design opening, not a new route.

Positive observer transport preserves both symbolic admission and actual execution and therefore
cannot manufacture an execution gap. A noninjective triangle observer can nevertheless erase a
real source gap while creating false positive overlap. Observer collapse is therefore another
one-way information-loss mechanism, not a repair producer.

### Relative formation-completion current [ROUTE-V CANDIDATE — NOT INSTANTIATED]

A dependency-closed paper theorem now identifies the exact information lost by centered reflection

Its positive route-design survivor is precise: genuinely new law may live in irreducible

### External Zeta-23 finite-compression density theorem

Claude's 10 August 2026 paper *More than two thirds of the zeros of the Riemann zeta function lie
on the critical line*, with its public `zeta-23-lean` artifact, adds a genuine unconditional
finite-Weil theorem: critical-density Gabor compressions, unconditional prime-side trace and
Frobenius-square asymptotics, and a multiplicity-aware Hermitian rank--trace inequality yield
critical-line/simple proportions `2/3` and distinct proportion `5/6` (optimized to approximately
`0.6725`, `0.6725`, `0.83625`). The external Lean artifact states these results directly against
Mathlib's `riemannZeta`; local source audit found no forbidden declarations under its theorem
library, while independent replay of its different Lean/Mathlib toolchain has not been performed in
this corpus. Detailed disposition is in `claude-zeta23-rank-trace-audit.md`.

This does not add a lettered RH route or close the R/L/CCM producer. The scalar compression has
already codiagonalized prime labels and reflection orientations, and its normalized trace moments
cannot exclude one, finitely many, or density-zero off-line blocks. That limitation coincides with
the corpus's formal `MeasuredIndexBlindness` wall.

A hostile follow-up closes the proposed moment-neutral amplification escape. The source-horizontal
prime tail already amplifies every off-line multiplicity copy into arbitrarily many distinct,
nonzero, reflection-faithful hyperbolic blocks with arbitrarily small average Frobenius-square cost;
this is formalized in `SourceHorizontalMomentNeutralAmplification`. It cannot contradict the
paper's inequality: each block adds one positive-index unit, and the rank--trace certificate allows
`c^2` per such unit. The paper's equality examples make that price sharp. Requiring every block to
remain above a fixed spectral threshold forces at least `2 delta^2` Frobenius square per block, so
sublinear moment growth is possible only through spectral collapse. Bounded source operations
preserve the collapse; unbounded sign/projection normalization has simultaneous forward/reflected
domain exactly at neutral grade and therefore restates the existing RH-equivalent producer. Detailed
proof and conjecture loop: `source-horizontal-moment-neutral-amplification-no-go.md`.

The umbrella-reachable shared-reservoir successor preserves every prime coefficient while coupling
all blocks through one negative reservoir. It realizes exact opposite inertias with arbitrarily
small Frobenius square, but also proves that an off-line packet and its reflection cannot both
embed quadratic-form-faithfully in any coefficient-generated global Stokes Witt form: that target
has only one negative port. Thus the amplification data now yield a sharp Route-R landing
criterion, not a contradiction with the finite-compression density theorem.

## 6. Fundamental nature of remaining problems

Across live routes, first unresolved statements fall into five mathematical classes.

| Class | Routes | Missing knowledge |
|---|---|---|
| singular source descent | C1, L, R, CCM-FU | positive closure or coefficient-faithful annihilation before scalar quotient; R/L additionally need a constitutive coefficient selector because vertical-gauge invariance erases and marked splitting is arbitrary |
| all-prime oscillatory transport | D2, E, T | cancellation or finite ceiling retaining arithmetic phase and physical normalization; T's safe symbol, Wold pole formation, and mesh-gap equivalence are formal, leaving continuation/noncancellation of the complete identity toward the physical wall |
| adaptive spectral orientation | G, U | source-conditioned sign on moving boundary/null data; U's first singular face and every fixed positive even contact are Lean-classified, leaving cofinal contact-order/degree drift |
| whole-space coercivity | O | classify all low-energy ground-space escape directions, not one test vector; N's analogous receiver has been terminalized |
| positive carrier existence | P, Q | build literal compact measure after coupled pole--zeta--Gamma cancellation |

This explains repeated stalls. Classical functional analysis supplies receivers once sign,
boundedness, or coercivity is present. Corpus no-gos show these properties are not inherited from
ambient positivity, faithful re-encoding, local exactness, finite moments, bounded observer
towers, isometry, compact-carrier representation, or determinant packaging. The shared horizontal
carrier theorem sharpens this diagnosis: retained class and its orientation are already present;
missing theorem is an arithmetic law forcing its annihilation. More generally, decisive theorem
must act on source before completion removes reflection-odd, phase-sensitive, or adaptive boundary
coordinate. The exact longitudinal--transverse law sharpens “source selection” further: decisive
operation must mix zero-codiagonal source information into longitudinal divisor response; a
radical-preserving intervention can only rescale what was already there.

## 7. Retired mechanism ledger

The following constructions are closed at advertised strength:

- finite local selectors and bounded label-blind codiagonals;
- universal Gaussian/CCR/Fock positivity without source incidence;
- finite Hankel or determinant positivity presented as all-degree source positivity;
- fixed-width rectangular Weil floors;
- finite-height Sonin transfer and compact multiplicative repairs;
- one-angle entire Suzuki characteristic convergence;
- finite-index parity/Pontryagin comparison for Suzuki kernel;
- fixed finite Wold moments and polynomial seam patching as Möbius cancellation;
- Route-E arguments using only generalized-prime local decomposition, PNT/Mertens windows, or a
  finite ordinary-prime prefix;
- finite positive amplifier patterns as a uniform boundary bridge: their certified exclusion
  width is capped at inverse scale;
- scalar phase nodes and finite differential-algebraic prime-germ readers;
- unrenormalized Julia energy, post hoc diagonal subtraction, and bounded residual polarization;
- separable prime-depth incidence and vertex-gauge cycle holonomy;
- fixed clock-jet spectra, unordered scalar frequency fusion, and positive Gamma shorting;
- target-pulled metrics, ordinary Cartier exactness, and generic mapping cones as R/L producers;
- abstract cohomological duality, period-one Stokes normalization, scaling/Haar invariance, or
  reflection alone as a Route-R/L physical-coefficient selector;
- full vertical-gauge invariance as nonerasing descent: it annihilates the retained prime class,
  while choosing a gauge splitting leaves every coefficient possible;
- existence of an ordinary complex retaining Route-R raw curvature: universal stabilization
  supplies one at every grade, so naturality/minimality/admission must exclude it;
- closed/closable Calderón--Witt extensions or multivalued closed relations which agree with the
  old zero boundary on the dense arithmetic core;
- compact-orbit periodization or rotation-invariant cross-prime compact energy as Route-L
  producer: cofinal labels separate algebraically but compact topology erases noncompact escape;
- positive/passive completion of the centered `Lambda-1` Route-T current, and linear co-moving
  aperture extinction as a faithful mass detector;
- bounded same-carrier realization of Route T's physical `p+1` observer;
- reflected exterior theta packets as two independent Route-T quadratures: they are exact
  negatives with equal norm;
- one common Banach-algebra formation receiver carrying a bounded derivation with `D T=1`;
- algebraic prime augmentation as a new symplectic obstruction: reduction is only one hyperbolic
  plane and supplies no arithmetic Witt-rank producer;
- Hilbert or `c0` completion as a faithful all-prime one-port receiver: the augmentation constraint
  is dense and Hausdorff reduction erases the port; only the `ell1` phase preserves it. Finite
  phase recurrence and complete finite jet tomography do not repair this, and the global
  unweighted observer has no admitted infinitesimal generator;
- transverse complete-shell perturbations or radical-preserving interventions as a new divisor
  selector;
- first-order displacement tests of a complete functional-equation quadruple;
- new seed asymptotics or finite real-zero determinants as substitute for O whole-ground control;
- uniqueness/determinacy as substitute for P carrier existence;
- positive Gamma orientation, cumulant sign, fixed angular sector, or endpoint height as substitute
  for U adaptive crossing sign.

Every retired route remains retired unless new work changes primitive or defeats its exact no-go.

## 8. Literature exchange and novelty boundary

Primary literature supplies parent languages but not current Tier-1 source theorems.

- Hedenmalm's 2026 differential-pair spectral realization gives a new spectral interpretation of
  zeta zeros, but no T outgoing-current bound, N source-domain estimate, or U null-flag
  orientation: https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17494
- Suzuki's 2026 screw-function formulation gives finite-interval self-adjoint operators and a
  conjectural limiting program, but no source-selected G transfer or U completed-source crossing
  sign: https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09096
- Berg--Szwarc characterize Hankel determinant/positivity subtleties; determinant nonnegativity
  does not supply PSD of every adaptive U face: https://arxiv.org/abs/1405.7267
- Salazar obtains total positivity from positive moment representations; this does not orient
  signed adaptive discrepancy after shifted-Xi factorial normalization:
  https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31647
- Matomäki--Radziwiłł--Tao's averaged Chowla/Elliott theorem and Menon's 2026 refinement give the
  relevant external correlation technology. Lean now proves the required small/large truncation
  bridge without a maximum over all endpoints. Remaining import is the exact uniform Möbius
  specialization, including nonpretentious hypotheses and rates, of the published theorem:
  https://arxiv.org/abs/1503.05121 and https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15574

Scoped searches found no exact external collision for T's complete mass-light-cone reduction or
U's Hilbert--charge/null-flag/Lorentz chain. This is evidence for corpus novelty, not proof of
historical priority. Specialist review remains required.

External results worth formalizing must be unconditional, source-specific, absent from corpus, and
must narrow first open premise. Additional abstract RH criteria, generic spectral models, or
moment theorems do not meet this bar.

## 9. Disproof frontier

One rigorously certified off-line Xi zero disproves RH. Equivalent finite negative witnesses are
already formalized through restricted-Weil floors, continued prime--archimedean tails, Hankel
faces, and several determinant criteria. No such witness exists in corpus.

Most explicit source-level disproof candidate is growing-order squared-Xi reverse-Bessel radial
jet. Orders through eighteen are positive; moving-prime comparison from order nineteen onward is
open. Numerical evidence is not accepted as proof or disproof.

## 10. Research priorities

### Shortest live chains

1. **R:** construct a nonfunctorial positive polar reduction from the full numerical Euler--Gamma
   source, then derive the genuinely global coefficient-faithful law identifying its
   source-exact reflected self-product with
   `4 n^(-2)sinh(a log(n)/2)^2`, equivalently force nonabelian centrality of the already-oriented
   horizontal carrier. Exact target is source-observer discriminant transversality: both
   reflection projections nonzero under a constitutive coefficient selector.
   Mere complex formation is vacuous; full vertical-gauge invariance erases the class, marked
   Stokes splitting leaves coefficient arbitrary, and scalar causal Green or local same-fibre
   anomaly repairs erase the physical residue they would need to preserve. Linear Poisson/two-
   endpoint symmetry retains a theta shear, while bounded positive coinvariant pairings and
   invariant means erase localized atoms. Normal KMS weights retain the anomaly; the singular
   residue kills it but its Bockstein finite part is `(log n)/n`. C33 proves every
   source-automorphism-natural topology, polarization, relative-domain predicate, pure index, and
   local holomorphic/algebraic scalar invariant is hyperbolic-gauge blind. The new metric/domain
   must therefore be a named nonfunctorial polar choice derived from extra arithmetic, not a
   recombination of pre-polar invariants. The neutral-jet bridge is useful only through Route T's
   full residual upper-abscissa control.
2. **L:** lift the formalized exponent-exceptional marked-tame comparison from one divisor
   coefficient to the complete shell-amplified corrected Gamma-six source modulo trace class.
   Prove this global place-marked mixed `L1/L2` Rost--Schmid-to-Meyer map preserves the trace-class
   remainder, sends both exceptional branches to the two source-forced critical-edge Köthe slots,
   intertwines the full source boundary, and makes the fixed Burnol/Deninger reader cyclic there.
   Source object `zeta(s)/zeta(zs)`, actual-multiplicity exceptional profile, reflected pole/Green
   displacement, local boundary `[6^m]-[6^(-m)]`, exact all-grade Gamma/Burnol/BV character, and
   pointwise completed incidence landing are now closed and Lean-formalized. Tame reciprocity gives
   only product neutrality; the local incidence vector does not preserve the complete shell ideal.
   This global ideal-preserving lift and cyclicity are equivalently the remaining source-derived
   proof of `Omega_(mu,Gamma)(w_23)=0`. Counterterm shape, principal germs, full-grade clock,
   detector, Poisson-range kernel, strict provenance, observer-freedom no-gos, ambient open-strip
   admission, and exact critical-edge zero locus are closed.
3. **T:** prove signed/coherent continuation of the centered residual-plus-mesh package to the
   critical wall while retaining critical Wold residues, or complete Maynard's aggregate
   `R/R*` large-values argument for mesh energy and pair it with a non-absolute residual boundary
   law. Endpoint absolute variation and the two-sided graph norm are formally impossible; one
   small positive height remains insufficient for full RH.
4. **U:** close one of two literal branches. Degenerate/root-sign branch: prove the positive-axis
   selected Padé-pole measures
   `sum_(u_i<0)(omega_i/x_i)delta_(-u_i)` vanish vaguely on compact positive intervals, or prove an
   equivalent Poisson-boundary estimate. A simple negative hard root with positive terminal mass
   forces an atom converging to mass `gamma_r/F'(r)^2`; zero terminal mass forces extra even and
   odd radical. Hard-node velocity and all flat-contact jets are exact, and prove contact sign
   controls orientation but not endpoint level. Full odd-sector admission additionally needs
   complete form-domain liminf control. Simple-birth branch still needs a new literal prime--Gamma
   sign or equivalent global completed-Xi anchor. Unknown-mode classification, further root
   division, contact parity, fixed-degree arcsine recession, and generic Laguerre--Polya theory are
   closed. Amplitude/coordinate gauges, one-block capacity, least-defect parabolic scale,
   translation escape, boundary hyperbolicity, and simple-root Cauchy orthogonality remain closed
   at their stated levels.
   Alternate current-admission lane is now formally reduced to a label-sensitive
   prime--Gamma/global-Xi bound on the exact odd defect-energy/current-norm-flux expression.
   Fixed-mass transport is exact and explicit admission velocity cancels; a positive three-atom
   resolvent-flow countermodel rules out every argument using only finite Hankel positivity,
   canonical admission, smooth compact transport, and the generic moment ODE.
   Fixed-degree recession landing and the arithmetic beat-quartic collapse target are now
   umbrella-formal. The exact line-reflection module further identifies their stationary defect
   with the R/L same-carrier wall. None replaces the required cofinal/full-ground Xi input.
5. **E:** prove finite physical weighted projection
   `d_E^2 <= X^(-1+o(1))`, equivalently
   `N * Var_(nu/N)(rho/nu) <= X^(o(1))` or all-reporter minimum-neighbor expansion, then
   normalized seam flux and recurrence accumulation for fixed
   entropy-complement relation. Finite mixture, corrected load threshold, parity, and endpoint
   consumer are formal. Exact normalized weighted Hall is zero-energy stronger route; one-sided
   `1+o(1)` load is rate-insufficient. Universal Fisher contraction is rate-excluded as producer:
   forced
   shell clock is logarithmic and gauge-neutralized energy grows. Separately formalize the
   published averaged-Chowla theorem
   with the exact Möbius specialization, or prove the direct Gallagher lane's now-isolated
   `OffDiagonalSmall mu hh` premise. Its fixed/cofinally low-height and rough parts are closed; the analytic
   target is the coprime Möbius near-diagonal covariance hidden inside the Ramaré off-diagonal in
   the moving range `delta_M^(-2/3) << T << M`. The new ternary-marker mean-square theorem handles
   the finite marker cube unconditionally in Lean, including exact alternating coefficients,
   reciprocal Gibbs weights, cofinal antiscale saturation, canonical vanishing quadratic mass, and
   the actual coprime Möbius-product bridge. Statewise rough-cofactor flatness has now been reduced
   to one centered diffuse codiagonal reporter, and its exact physical energy is weighted
   marginal-projection distance. Prove cofactor tensoring plus all-reporter rate or
   recurrence-compatible collision defect, with same moving seam. Maximum-state collision and mean
   Euler component are already bounded; prime spacing, finite marker dissipation, square-multiple
   control, universal Fisher corner identification, critical-clock parameter choice, and
   strict-right graph normalization are not live gaps.
   Alternate direct lane: instantiate grown Lean conductance on actual recurrence kernel `P` and
   prove weighted-isolated mass `<=X^(1/2+o(1))` and inverse row-plus-column-load Dirichlet
   energy `E_P<=X^(o(1))` on full physical entropy-complement graph. One-sign restriction gives
   co-moving reverse chi-square, equivalent at strict-half scale, so retain parity-agnostic rows
   and prove common-aperture closure.
   Co-moving contribution already has correct size; symmetrization needs no Metropolis overlap or
   detailed balance. Biregular block estimates and bounded-prime local replacement graph are
   fenced.
6. **C1/CCM-FU:** prove a source-derived real-spectrum/positive-closure theorem or
   intersection/Ward incidence. Determinant, essential-trace, weighted-derivative,
   shifted-cokernel, and supersymmetric capstones are already certified. Extend the
   now-Lean-closed `m = 2` operator residue law (relative one-step and double-defect estimates hold
   for every `m >= 2`) to `m >= 3` via the k-fold defect chains, then prove the global trace identity
   that forces the already-positive Gauss--Hodge mass to vanish and identify it with the actual
   Weil/intersection object. The pointwise mass gap inside `xiWeilPolarization`, virial
   normalization, all-prime summability classification, GNS socket, Krein signature decomposition,
   and generic inverse-response Ward law are formal. Direct reflection-pair square completion is
   terminal because its negative sector is exactly the Hodge mass; missing producer is the
   arithmetic compressed-trace/anomalous-Ward identity. Perform
   widening-limit assembly only for parallel connector branches.
7. **O:** prove a literal source-side subexponential upper bound for the actual negative
   restricted-Weil floor. Profile exhaustion/bulk coercivity is the P-producer reduction; the
   completed Suzuki/Evans and noncompact incidence/direct-floor producers remain independent.
8. **G:** first settle both literal source-admission branches: exclude or prove the source-visible
   ground-residue reflection cone, then resolve the singular negative zero-shift boundary layer
   `r->0` above `R_Xi>81/4`. The former polylogarithmic rate is retracted because its growing
   low-block Gram minimum was uncontrolled. The normalized-Hankel classification leaves maximal ground-edge collapse,
   nonvanishing edge overshoot, or near-minimal forced source alignment; isolated microlocal prime
   seams are now closed, so an interior survivor must be delocalized and coherently multi-seam.
   Macroscopic, unbounded, and bounded-away-from-zero negative schedules are classified out for a
   complete family; no further recession or ordinary finite-window analysis replaces the
   source-aligned growing-Gram small-singular-value problem.
   Only after admission, prove compactified even/odd occupancy convergence/every paired smoothing
   trace for a source-selected adjacent/interior branch.  One trace moment and regular
   vector/resolvent convergence are insufficient.
9. **D2:** obtain a literal high-frequency ceiling for the continued prime--Gamma source. Route N
   is a terminalized receiver, not a parallel live task.
10. **P/Q:** construct P's compact positive carrier or prove Q's direct Cassini--Jensen/jet
    saturation. Q's selected continuous power-reciprocal carrier is retired; any reuse requires a
    source-derived discontinuous plateau quotient rather than all-degree scalar closure.

### Work unlikely to move frontier

- more RH-equivalent scalar coordinates;
- finite-prefix positivity;
- generic compactness without source admission;
- more Route O seed asymptotics without ground-space classification;
- more Route E moments or seam patch algebra without unit physical incidence;
- more Route-E/GHS mean-value, fibre-kernel, or maximal-iteration packaging without either the
  published averaged-Chowla premise, `OffDiagonalSmall mu hh`, or rate-sharp physical
  weighted-projection/all-reporter law;
- more fixed-height/PNT-Abel or rough-number estimates for Route E without entering the remaining
  prime off-diagonal Ramaré/Halász range;
- more Route R frames or orientation detectors without independent singular source annihilation;
- more Route U positive carriers, cumulant signs, fourth-cumulant reserve restatements, or sector
  bounds without precompression adaptive source orientation;
- builds or formalization of conjectures whose mathematical source theorem is not proved.

obstruction, Green-sum range theorem, finite depletion tower, converse, exact-kernel,
Euler-depleted-kernel, Meyer-topology, and continuous-moment files receive formal credit.

smoothness, exact safe-side symbol, Wold pole continuation, and prime-gap quadrature are
umbrella-reachable; their listed declarations receive formal credit.

### Route S and cross-cutting arithmetic source infrastructure

`ClosedHyperplaneInverse.lean`, `CompactHyperplanePerturbation.lean`,
`PolynomialCircuitParametrisation.lean`, and `CircuitExceptionalTimes.lean` are umbrella-reachable
at this cutoff. `ModulusBudget`, `ModulusDivergence`, `AdaptiveEigenframeTwoQuerySourceMemory`,
`CoeffDenominatorBound`, the complete Eisenstein chain,
`CriticalSchurIntegralClosurePlace`, the primitive-execution chain, the defect ladder, vacuum
bound, Newton--Puiseux theorem, graded-genesis capstone, symbolic-execution gap, and the other
anchors identified as wired below receive ordinary formal credit.
`CofinalInverseCapacity.lean`, its Hilbert and weighted-port descendants, and
`TorusInvariantField.lean` are umbrella-reachable at this cutoff.

coefficient-faithful Gamma-six shell.  `SourceHorizontalGammaSixAbelianStripSlack.lean` proves that
this fusion still evacuates every multiplicity-labelled Xi divisor copy.  With
`a=|2 Re(rho)-1|<1`, sharp geometric majorants have bases

```text
forward: 6^(2a-2) = 6^(-2(1-a)),
reverse: 6^(a-2).
```

Thus shell rank does not replenish source admission anywhere in the open Xi strip.  Forward
orientation is exact bottleneck: its residual exponent is twice strip slack.  This yields a new
producer invariant: **strip slack is renewal codimension**.  Any successful coefficient-faithful
source injection into this carrier must contribute at least two prime-clock exponent units on the
forward branch (or replace multiplicative renewal by a non-mass-preserving source law).  Ordinary
flags cannot do this because they preserve probability mass; Big-Witt relocation cannot do it
because corpus already proves its sharp per-index cost.  This closes the literal fusion while
narrowing the missing source theorem quantitatively; it is not an RH proof.

### Frankenstein seam: reflected Stokes subtraction = two-clock information coenergy

The quantitative Gamma-six obstruction now has one literal cross-route realization.  Pair the
P-G55 prime-power information virial at the functional-equation complementary coordinate
`t=(1-s)/2` with P-G70's reflected Stokes weight on the **same** `(p,e)` label.  The unconditional
module `SourceHorizontalStokesConditionedInformationCoenergy.lean` proves

```text
0 < J_s(p,e)
  <= 4(1-s) p^(-2) (p^(-s))^(2e) / (e+1)^2,
0 < s < 1,
```

and summability over every prime and valuation depth, with strictly positive total coenergy.  This
is an exact coefficient-faithful recovery of the two exponent units demanded by renewal
codimension: the information factor contributes `r^(e+1)`, reflected inverse-gap susceptibility
contributes `r^e/r`, and their product retains `r^(2e)` while the Möbius aperture contributes the
universal `p^(-2)` envelope.  P-G58's divergent information observable and P-G70's finite compact
carrier are therefore not separate facts; one source subtraction is the missing reciprocal
coenergy payment.

This does **not** close RH or rank--trace amplification.  The now-sharp open core is a completed
prime--Gamma seam identity placing this positive coenergy against a source-forced zero or opposite
sign.  More damping, summability, carrier construction, or abstract Herglotz realization is not
progress on that gate.

## Final frontier statement

Corpus has constructed source, completed landing, many positive geometries, and exact RH
receivers. It also proves why bounded label-blind completion, generic positivity, local exactness,
finite moments, isometry, and determinant packaging erase or fail to orient RH-bearing
coordinate.

Current frontier consists of eleven genuinely different lettered source problems plus the
unlettered CCM-FU incidence problem, concentrated in singular descent, oscillatory all-prime
transport, adaptive boundary orientation, whole-space coercivity, and positive carrier existence.
Routes T and U are real additions. Source-horizontal,
Burnol, eigen-pair, centered-prime-tail, Gamma--Lorentz, and universal-law-field additions narrow
existing routes or strengthen receiver/no-go infrastructure; they do not add another lettered
route. The Euler q-Gamma/abelian-gas process is a genuine new pre-zero source-to-divisor
architecture, now
including formal global class-law, expectation, depth-divisor, KMS uniqueness, RG-dilation, and
natural-boundary calculus in addition to relative-entropy evaluation, positive GNS/C-star
realization and C-star-level state uniqueness, hypertranscendence, and zero-free genus-one erasure,
the complete modulus-defect ladder, wired modulus budget and prime-window nonsummability, and
nonsummable condensed-phase symmetrization,
but it lacks a critical-line orientation and RH handoff, so it remains a candidate rather than
Route V. Its marked parameter commutator, socle translation/holomorphy/hypertranscendence, and
complete socle-divisor order are formal; fugacity entropy and recurrence/open lift remain open. The
two-source exchange-versus-mixed-transgression noncollapse is formal, but also has no RH handoff.
Five additional Route-V candidates are now mathematically substantial.
The conservative Euler-shell/Witt completion has an exact formal receiver and a Lean
RH-equivalent pointwise selection gate but lacks the
literal jointly convergent source/exterior inverse pair; one common Banach receiver is formally
excluded. Its marked-shell positive GNS/HT realization is also formal, but the normalization wall
separates every summable positive shell (`sigma>1`) from the physical half-density exponent
(`sigma=1/m<=1/12`). The triptic/elliptic-gamma branch has a complete orbital-divisor normal form and finite
realization theorem, now with exact additive and multiplicative kernel converses, but lacks a
coefficient-faithful Xi handoff and reflection orientation. Tropical-projective source-normal
descent has exact prime-clock projective width and recurrence, but lacks an Xi-typed observer and
descent theorem. Relative formation-completion has an exact umbrella-reachable centered-jet
quotient/evenization core, but lacks its literal arithmetic relative extension and
source-normal hidden-current annihilation law. None satisfies the corpus definition of an
instantiated route.
The Helson branch has coefficient-faithful trace-class realization and exact simple Fredholm-wall
response, but lacks a source-derived Xi stream, fixed RH-equivalent wall, multiple-crossing
control, and global orientation/avoidance theorem.

The Gauss-component residue hierarchy is an adjacent local Connes candidate whose actual circle
operators, principal factorization, finite iteration, full two-frequency diagonal, sandwich fold,
relative one-step bound, rank-one operator logarithmic residue, and `m>=2` ratio-weighted
translation estimate, general half-power tail closure, literal principal-step identity, and
operator-power telescope are umbrella-reachable, and the first propagated-iterate depth is
closed: the relative one-step and double-defect estimates hold in umbrella Lean for every
`m >= 2`, giving the operator logarithmic-mean residue law at `m = 2`. The label-faithful
multi-place assembly is also umbrella-reachable, with per-label residue laws for the actual
assembled operator at `m <= 2`, exact codiagonal collapse onto the paired scalar, an injective
cofinal canonical aperture, and a carrier-compatible positive residue current. Its Hodge mass
pairing is now nonnegative, reflection-even, and has exact critical radical; vanishing is RH.
The GNS socket and Krein decomposition now show exactly why local sign is insufficient: the Weil
form is unconditionally pair-sum energy minus reflection-difference energy, and eliminating the
negative sector is itself equivalent to RH and Hodge-mass vanishing. Local sign and indefinite
realization are therefore closed; direct reflection-pair square completion is terminal. The
missing theorem is an independent arithmetic global trace/intersection identity
forcing that positive mass to vanish, not another pairing construction. Deeper
propagated-iterate control (`m >= 3`),
identification with the actual Weil/intersection object, and that global annihilating trace remain.
Route L's cofinal prime-period tomography proves algebraic label sufficiency and topological
non-strictness; its formal Floquet, Steinberg, Gamma-six, and Mobius-successor layers prove the
missing object is the global ideal-preserving lift from the complete shell-amplified singular
source to the fixed Gamma/co-Poisson target, not another source normal, compact periodization, or
scalar diagonal form. Route O now has two internal producer branches: whole-ground
control beyond the prolate seed, and the completed-zero-mode-to-support-pruned-carrier intertwiner
beyond the closed weak Gamma and root-port steps. Route P has formal P-G79--P-G90 source geometry
and a committed P-G91 local covariance theorem pending umbrella certification, but still lacks
non-diagonal coupled-pole completed Gamma/polar Feshbach realization.
Route T's Wold-side wall is a signed/distributional pole-preserving landing; its arithmetic wall has
moved past Perron relevance, finite incidence formation, recurrence solution, actual-interval
embedding, logarithmic windows, Taylor control, finite convolution, and polynomial bilinear saving
to a strict one-dimensional phase-block estimate. Its next wall is MV24.11--24.12 near-one
transport. Route U's terminal wall is unconditional orientation of its explicit countable nodal
adaptive arithmetic landings at one shift. Its effective
descent, hard-edge worldline, flat-contact transmutation, adaptive priced renewals, and degree-

The coalitional/collective-repair/cofinal-viability laws, primitive-degree escape, and observer
execution-gap laws expose a second route-design primitive, but no arithmetic repair family or Xi
handoff exists. Exact Hedenmalm source spectrum, determinant/Riesz structure, essential
inverse-flow/Weil package, squared Cayley receiver, closed weighted derivative,
shifted-cokernel/source equation, and supersymmetric Dirac are now one umbrella-reachable C1
opening. The direct-Cayley/two-port extension is now umbrella-reachable and remains a receiver; it
does not define an independent route.
Shilov matrix/tower and the now-complete commutative C*-envelope/boundary-representation model,
cofinal Gamma relation law, congruence-symbol no-go, and constitutive repair laws strengthen
receiver and obstruction bookkeeping, not source production.
Nyman--Beurling finite sections likewise strengthen a classical receiver, while the generalized
Beurling system layer is a Route-E firewall rather than a new producer.

RH remains open. Any claim of progress must now prove one named source theorem above, refute one
route with an exact no-go and positive survivor, or construct a literal new source architecture
with an unconditional handoff not source-isogenous to an existing route.
